### [221] [Zero-shot Low-Field MRI Enhancement via Diffusion-Based Adaptive Contrast Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01913)
*Muyu Liu,Chenhe Du,Xuanyu Tian,Qing Wu,Xiao Wang,Haonan Zhang,Hongjiang Wei,Yuyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DACT：基于扩散的自适应对比度传输框架，无需配对监督从低场MRI恢复高场质量图像


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低场MRI虽然普及了诊断成像，但受限于低信噪比和场依赖弛豫动态导致的组织对比度失真。从低场数据重建高场质量图像是一个盲逆问题，面临配对训练数据稀缺和未知非线性对比度变换算子的挑战。现有零样本方法假设简化的线性退化，往往无法恢复真实的组织对比度。

Method: 提出DACT（基于扩散的自适应对比度传输）框架，结合预训练的高场扩散先验确保解剖保真度与物理信息自适应前向模型。引入可微分的Sinkhorn最优传输模块，在反向扩散过程中显式建模和校正低场与高场域之间的强度分布偏移，动态学习难处理的对比度映射同时保持拓扑一致性。

Result: 在模拟和真实临床低场数据集上的广泛实验表明，DACT实现了最先进的性能，重建结果具有优越的结构细节和正确的组织对比度。

Conclusion: DACT是一个创新的零样本框架，能够有效解决低场MRI重建中的对比度失真问题，无需配对监督即可恢复高场质量图像，为低场MRI的临床应用提供了重要技术支持。

Abstract: Low-field (LF) magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) democratizes access to diagnostic imaging but is fundamentally limited by low signal-to-noise ratio and significant tissue contrast distortion due to field-dependent relaxation dynamics. Reconstructing high-field (HF) quality images from LF data is a blind inverse problem, severely challenged by the scarcity of paired training data and the unknown, non-linear contrast transformation operator. Existing zero-shot methods, which assume simplified linear degradation, often fail to recover authentic tissue contrast. In this paper, we propose DACT(Diffusion-Based Adaptive Contrast Transport), a novel zero-shot framework that restores HF-quality images without paired supervision. DACT synergizes a pre-trained HF diffusion prior to ensure anatomical fidelity with a physically-informed adaptive forward model. Specifically, we introduce a differentiable Sinkhorn optimal transport module that explicitly models and corrects the intensity distribution shift between LF and HF domains during the reverse diffusion process. This allows the framework to dynamically learn the intractable contrast mapping while preserving topological consistency. Extensive experiments on simulated and real clinical LF datasets demonstrate that DACT achieves state-of-the-art performance, yielding reconstructions with superior structural detail and correct tissue contrast.

</details>


### [222] [LaST-VLA: Thinking in Latent Spatio-Temporal Space for Vision-Language-Action in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01928)
*Yuechen Luo,Fang Li,Shaoqing Xu,Yang Ji,Zehan Zhang,Bing Wang,Yuannan Shen,Jianwei Cui,Long Chen,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Zhi-Xin Yang,Fuxi Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LaST-VLA提出了一种基于潜在时空链式思维的VLA框架，通过双特征对齐机制将几何约束和动态预见融入潜在空间，在自动驾驶任务中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA模型依赖显式文本链式思维，导致语义-感知解耦和感知-符号冲突；现有潜在推理方法缺乏物理基础，成为物理不可知的表示。

Method: 提出潜在时空VLA框架，通过双特征对齐机制从3D基础模型提取几何约束、从世界模型提取动态预见到潜在空间；采用渐进式SFT训练策略，从特征对齐过渡到轨迹生成，并通过GRPO强化学习进行安全性和规则合规性优化。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上达到91.3 PDMS，NAVSIM v2上达到87.1 EPDMS的新记录；在SURDS和NuDynamics基准测试中表现出卓越的时空推理能力。

Conclusion: LaST-VLA通过将推理范式从离散符号处理转向物理基础的潜在时空链式思维，解决了VLA模型中的语义-感知解耦问题，实现了更物理感知的自动驾驶推理。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have revolutionized autonomous driving by unifying perception and planning, their reliance on explicit textual Chain-of-Thought (CoT) leads to semantic-perceptual decoupling and perceptual-symbolic conflicts. Recent shifts toward latent reasoning attempt to bypass these bottlenecks by thinking in continuous hidden space. However, without explicit intermediate constraints, standard latent CoT often operates as a physics-agnostic representation. To address this, we propose the Latent Spatio-Temporal VLA (LaST-VLA), a framework shifting the reasoning paradigm from discrete symbolic processing into a physically grounded Latent Spatio-Temporal CoT. By implementing a dual-feature alignment mechanism, we distill geometric constraints from 3D foundation models and dynamic foresight from world models directly into the latent space. Coupled with a progressive SFT training strategy that transitions from feature alignment to trajectory generation, and refined via Reinforcement Learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to ensure safety and rule compliance. \method~setting a new record on NAVSIM v1 (91.3 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (87.1 EPDMS), while excelling in spatial-temporal reasoning on SURDS and NuDynamics benchmarks.

</details>


### [223] [MobileMold: A Smartphone-Based Microscopy Dataset for Food Mold Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01944)
*Dinh Nam Pham,Leonard Prokisch,Bennet Meyer,Jonas Thumbs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MobileMold是一个开源的智能手机显微镜数据集，用于食品霉菌检测和食品分类，包含4941张手持显微镜图像，涵盖11种食品类型、4款智能手机和3种显微镜，在真实条件下采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机夹式显微镜将日常设备转变为低成本、便携的成像系统，能够揭示微观层面的真菌结构，实现超越肉眼检查的霉菌检测。研究旨在为可访问的食品安全传感、移动成像以及增强附件的智能手机潜力研究做出贡献。

Method: 创建了包含4941张手持显微镜图像的MobileMold数据集，涵盖11种食品类型、4款智能手机、3种显微镜和多样化的真实世界条件。建立了霉菌检测和食品类型分类的基准，包括预测两种属性的多任务设置。使用多种预训练深度学习架构和增强策略进行评估，并辅以基于显著性的视觉解释来突出与模型预测相关的霉菌区域。

Result: 在多个预训练深度学习架构和增强策略下，获得了接近上限的性能（准确率=0.9954，F1分数=0.9954，MCC=0.9907），验证了数据集在检测食品腐败方面的实用性。通过基于显著性的视觉解释增强了透明度，突出了与模型预测相关的霉菌区域。

Conclusion: MobileMold数据集为食品霉菌检测和分类提供了有价值的资源，展示了智能手机增强显微镜在食品安全传感和移动成像方面的潜力。该研究为可访问的食品安全监测和智能手机附件增强应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Smartphone clip-on microscopes turn everyday devices into low-cost, portable imaging systems that can even reveal fungal structures at the microscopic level, enabling mold inspection beyond unaided visual checks. In this paper, we introduce MobileMold, an open smartphone-based microscopy dataset for food mold detection and food classification. MobileMold contains 4,941 handheld microscopy images spanning 11 food types, 4 smartphones, 3 microscopes, and diverse real-world conditions. Beyond the dataset release, we establish baselines for (i) mold detection and (ii) food-type classification, including a multi-task setting that predicts both attributes. Across multiple pretrained deep learning architectures and augmentation strategies, we obtain near-ceiling performance (accuracy = 0.9954, F1 = 0.9954, MCC = 0.9907), validating the utility of our dataset for detecting food spoilage. To increase transparency, we complement our evaluation with saliency-based visual explanations highlighting mold regions associated with the model's predictions. MobileMold aims to contribute to research on accessible food-safety sensing, mobile imaging, and exploring the potential of smartphones enhanced with attachments.

</details>


### [224] [PreSight: Preoperative Outcome Prediction for Parkinson's Disease via Region-Prior Morphometry and Patient-Specific Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01948)
*Yand Wang,Chen Zhang,Lanyun Zhu,Yixin Chen,Qunbo Wang,Yutong Bai,Jurgen Germann,Yinghong Wen,Shuai Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PreSight：融合临床先验知识与患者特异性加权模块的术前预测模型，用于帕金森病手术预后评估，在双中心400例患者中表现优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病手术的术前预后预测具有重要临床意义但极具挑战性，主要因为影像信号微弱且患者异质性高。现有方法难以仅基于术前信息准确预测患者特异性术后运动功能改善。

Method: 提出PreSight模型，融合临床先验知识与术前MRI及形变基形态计量学（DBM），通过患者特异性加权模块自适应调整区域重要性，生成端到端、校准良好、可直接用于决策的预测结果，并提供患者层面的解释。

Result: 在双中心400例患者的多模态术前数据和术后改善标签上评估，PreSight在应答者分类任务中内部验证准确率达88.89%，外部中心测试达85.29%，优于临床基线、纯影像基线和多模态基线方法，展现出更好的概率校准和更高的决策曲线净收益。

Conclusion: 将临床先验知识与区域自适应形态计量学相结合，能够为常规临床实践提供可靠的术前决策支持，DBM和患者特异性加权模块对模型性能有重要贡献，模型能够以患者特异性方式关注疾病相关区域。

Abstract: Preoperative improvement rate prediction for Parkinson's disease surgery is clinically important yet difficult because imaging signals are subtle and patients are heterogeneous. We address this setting, where only information available before surgery is used, and the goal is to predict patient-specific postoperative motor benefit. We present PreSight, a presurgical outcome model that fuses clinical priors with preoperative MRI and deformation-based morphometry (DBM) and adapts regional importance through a patient-specific weighting module. The model produces end-to-end, calibrated, decision-ready predictions with patient-level explanations. We evaluate PreSight on a real-world two-center cohort of 400 subjects with multimodal presurgical inputs and postoperative improvement labels. PreSight outperforms strong clinical, imaging-only, and multimodal baselines. It attains 88.89% accuracy on internal validation and 85.29% on an external-center test for responder classification and shows better probability calibration and higher decision-curve net benefit. Ablations and analyses confirm the contribution of DBM and the patient-specific weighting module and indicate that the model emphasizes disease-relevant regions in a patient-specific manner. These results demonstrate that integrating clinical prior knowledge with region-adaptive morphometry enables reliable presurgical decision support in routine practice.

</details>


### [225] [Process Over Outcome: Cultivating Forensic Reasoning for Generalizable Multimodal Manipulation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01993)
*Yuchen Zhang,Yaxiong Wang,Kecheng Han,Yujiao Wu,Lianwei Wu,Li Zhu,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REFORM是一个基于推理的多模态媒体篡改检测框架，通过三阶段课程学习实现从结果拟合到过程建模的转变，在多个基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有篡改检测方法主要关注结果导向的篡改类型分类，缺乏可解释性且容易过拟合表面伪影。通用化检测需要显式的取证推理，而非仅仅分类有限的篡改类型。

Method: 提出REFORM框架，采用三阶段课程学习：1)诱导取证推理依据；2)对齐推理与最终判断；3)通过强化学习细化逻辑一致性。同时构建了ROM大规模数据集支持该范式。

Result: 在ROM数据集上达到81.52%准确率，在DGM4上达到76.65%准确率，在MMFakeBench上达到74.9 F1分数，建立了新的最先进性能并展现出优越的泛化能力。

Conclusion: REFORM通过推理驱动的学习范式显著提升了多模态媒体篡改检测的泛化能力和可解释性，为通用化检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have significantly enhanced the realism of multimodal media manipulation, thereby posing substantial challenges to manipulation detection. Existing manipulation detection and grounding approaches predominantly focus on manipulation type classification under result-oriented supervision, which not only lacks interpretability but also tends to overfit superficial artifacts. In this paper, we argue that generalizable detection requires incorporating explicit forensic reasoning, rather than merely classifying a limited set of manipulation types, which fails to generalize to unseen manipulation patterns. To this end, we propose REFORM, a reasoning-driven framework that shifts learning from outcome fitting to process modeling. REFORM adopts a three-stage curriculum that first induces forensic rationales, then aligns reasoning with final judgments, and finally refines logical consistency via reinforcement learning. To support this paradigm, we introduce ROM, a large-scale dataset with rich reasoning annotations. Extensive experiments show that REFORM establishes new state-of-the-art performance with superior generalization, achieving 81.52% ACC on ROM, 76.65% ACC on DGM4, and 74.9 F1 on MMFakeBench.

</details>


### [226] [Event-Only Drone Trajectory Forecasting with RPM-Modulated Kalman Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01997)
*Hari Prasanth S. M.,Pejman Habibiroudkenar,Eerik Alamikkotervo,Dimitrios Bouzoulas,Risto Ojala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于事件相机的无人机轨迹预测方法，利用螺旋桨运动线索，通过RPM感知卡尔曼滤波框架实现无需RGB图像或训练数据的准确预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率，适合观测快速移动的空中目标，但目前用于无人机轨迹预测的研究有限。现有方法通常依赖RGB图像或需要大量训练数据，而事件相机可直接捕捉螺旋桨旋转产生的运动线索，为轨迹预测提供新途径。

Method: 提出纯事件驱动的无人机预测方法：1）直接从原始事件数据中提取螺旋桨转速信息；2）将转速信息融合到RPM感知的卡尔曼滤波框架中；3）利用螺旋桨旋转产生的运动线索进行轨迹预测，无需RGB图像或训练数据。

Result: 在FRED数据集上的评估显示：该方法在0.4秒和0.8秒预测时间范围内，平均距离误差和最终距离误差均优于基于学习的方法和标准卡尔曼滤波器。证明了该方法在短中期轨迹预测中的鲁棒性和准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功利用事件相机的高时间分辨率特性，通过螺旋桨转速线索实现了准确的无监督轨迹预测。展示了事件相机在无人机跟踪和预测领域的潜力，提供了一种不依赖RGB图像或大量训练数据的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Event cameras provide high-temporal-resolution visual sensing that is well suited for observing fast-moving aerial objects; however, their use for drone trajectory prediction remains limited. This work introduces an event-only drone forecasting method that exploits propeller-induced motion cues. Propeller rotational speed are extracted directly from raw event data and fused within an RPM-aware Kalman filtering framework. Evaluations on the FRED dataset show that the proposed method outperforms learning-based approaches and vanilla kalman filter in terms of average distance error and final distance error at 0.4s and 0.8s forecasting horizons. The results demonstrate robust and accurate short- and medium-horizon trajectory forecasting without reliance on RGB imagery or training data.

</details>


### [227] [MAP-Diff: Multi-Anchor Guided Diffusion for Progressive 3D Whole-Body Low-Dose PET Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02012)
*Peiyuan Jing,Chun-Wun Cheng,Liutao Yang,Zhenxuan Zhang,Thiago V. Lima,Klaus Strobel,Antoine Leimgruber,Angelica Aviles-Rivero,Guang Yang,Javier A. Montoya-Zegarra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAP-Diff：一种用于渐进式3D全身PET去噪的多锚点引导扩散框架，通过引入临床观察的中等剂量扫描作为轨迹锚点，约束反向过程向剂量对齐的中间状态发展，显著提升低剂量PET图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量正电子发射断层扫描（PET）可减少辐射暴露，但存在严重噪声和定量退化问题。现有基于扩散的去噪模型虽然能获得良好的最终重建结果，但其反向轨迹通常不受约束，与PET剂量形成的渐进特性不一致。

Method: 提出MAP-Diff框架，引入临床观察的中等剂量扫描作为轨迹锚点，通过时间步相关的监督来正则化反向过程，使其向剂量对齐的中间状态发展。锚点时间步通过模拟扩散退化与真实多剂量PET对之间的退化匹配进行校准，并采用时间步加权的锚点损失来稳定分阶段学习。推理时仅需超低剂量输入即可实现渐进式、剂量一致的中间恢复。

Result: 在内部（西门子Biograph Vision Quadra）和跨扫描仪（联影uEXPLORER）数据集上均优于CNN、Transformer、GAN和扩散基线方法。内部数据集上，PSNR从42.48 dB提升至43.71 dB（+1.23 dB），SSIM达0.986，NMAE从0.115降至0.103（-0.012）。外部队列上获得34.42 dB PSNR和0.141 NMAE，优于所有竞争方法。

Conclusion: MAP-Diff通过多锚点引导的扩散框架有效解决了低剂量PET去噪问题，实现了渐进式、剂量一致的图像恢复，在内部和跨扫描仪数据集上均表现出优越性能，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Low-dose Positron Emission Tomography (PET) reduces radiation exposure but suffers from severe noise and quantitative degradation. Diffusion-based denoising models achieve strong final reconstructions, yet their reverse trajectories are typically unconstrained and not aligned with the progressive nature of PET dose formation. We propose MAP-Diff, a multi-anchor guided diffusion framework for progressive 3D whole-body PET denoising. MAP-Diff introduces clinically observed intermediate-dose scans as trajectory anchors and enforces timestep-dependent supervision to regularize the reverse process toward dose-aligned intermediate states. Anchor timesteps are calibrated via degradation matching between simulated diffusion corruption and real multi-dose PET pairs, and a timestep-weighted anchor loss stabilizes stage-wise learning. At inference, the model requires only ultra-low-dose input while enabling progressive, dose-consistent intermediate restoration. Experiments on internal (Siemens Biograph Vision Quadra) and cross-scanner (United Imaging uEXPLORER) datasets show consistent improvements over strong CNN-, Transformer-, GAN-, and diffusion-based baselines. On the internal dataset, MAP-Diff improves PSNR from 42.48 dB to 43.71 dB (+1.23 dB), increases SSIM to 0.986, and reduces NMAE from 0.115 to 0.103 (-0.012) compared to 3D DDPM. Performance gains generalize across scanners, achieving 34.42 dB PSNR and 0.141 NMAE on the external cohort, outperforming all competing methods.

</details>


### [228] [NICO-RAG: Multimodal Hypergraph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Understanding the Nicotine Public Health Crisis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02047)
*Manuel Serna-Aguilera,Raegan Anderes,Page Dobbs,Khoa Luu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NICO数据集包含20万+多模态样本（图像和文本），涵盖55个烟草/尼古丁品牌；NICO-RAG框架通过超图组织实体关系，实现无需语言模型高成本的图像检索，性能媲美SOTA图像RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烟草行业通过调味尼古丁等创新产品吸引年轻用户，削弱了反烟草运动成果。现有研究在数据规模和跨模态关联方面存在局限，需要大规模多模态数据集来支持公共卫生研究。

Method: 构建NICO数据集（20万+多模态样本，55个品牌）；提出NICO-RAG框架，通过超图组织图像和文本提取的实体关系，实现基于视觉和语义相似性的图像检索，避免语言模型的高计算成本。

Result: NICO-RAG在100多个问题上表现与最先进的图像RAG方法相当，但无需处理额外的图像token，显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: NICO数据集和NICO-RAG框架为公共卫生研究提供了大规模多模态数据资源和高效检索工具，有助于对抗烟草行业的创新营销策略。

Abstract: The nicotine addiction public health crisis continues to be pervasive. In this century alone, the tobacco industry has released and marketed new products in an aggressive effort to lure new and young customers for life. Such innovations and product development, namely flavored nicotine or tobacco such as nicotine pouches, have undone years of anti-tobacco campaign work. Past work is limited both in scope and in its ability to connect large-scale data points. Thus, we introduce the Nicotine Innovation Counter-Offensive (NICO) Dataset to provide public health researchers with over 200,000 multimodal samples, including images and text descriptions, on 55 tobacco and nicotine product brands. In addition, to provide public health researchers with factual connections across a large-scale dataset, we propose NICO-RAG, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that can retrieve image features without incurring the high-cost of language models, as well as the added cost of processing image tokens with large-scale datasets such as NICO. At construction time, NICO-RAG organizes image- and text-extracted entities and relations into hypergraphs to produce as factual responses as possible. This joint multimodal knowledge representation enables NICO-RAG to retrieve images for query answering not only by visual similarity but also by the semantic similarity of image descriptions. Experimentals show that without needing to process additional tokens from images for over 100 questions, NICO-RAG performs comparably to the state-of-the-art RAG method adapted for images.

</details>


### [229] [WorldStereo: Bridging Camera-Guided Video Generation and Scene Reconstruction via 3D Geometric Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02049)
*Yisu Zhang,Chenjie Cao,Tengfei Wang,Xuhui Zuo,Junta Wu,Jianke Zhu,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldStereo是一个连接相机引导视频生成与3D重建的新框架，通过两个几何记忆模块实现多视角一致视频生成和高质量3D重建


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视频扩散模型在生成视频质量上取得显著进展，但从生成视频重建一致3D场景仍然具有挑战性，主要问题在于相机可控性有限以及从不同相机轨迹观看时生成内容的不一致性

Method: 提出WorldStereo框架，包含两个几何记忆模块：全局几何记忆通过增量更新的点云实现精确相机控制并注入粗粒度结构先验；空间立体记忆通过3D对应关系约束注意力感受野，从记忆库中聚焦细粒度细节。采用基于控制分支的灵活架构，无需联合训练即可受益于分布匹配蒸馏的VDM骨干

Result: 在相机引导视频生成和3D重建基准测试中表现出有效性，能够生成多视角一致视频并实现高质量3D重建。WorldStereo可作为强大的世界模型，处理从透视或全景图像开始的多样化场景生成任务

Conclusion: WorldStereo通过创新的几何记忆模块成功桥接了相机引导视频生成与3D重建，实现了精确相机控制下的多视角一致性，为高质量3D场景重建提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Recent advances in foundational Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) have yielded significant progress. Yet, despite the remarkable visual quality of generated videos, reconstructing consistent 3D scenes from these outputs remains challenging, due to limited camera controllability and inconsistent generated content when viewed from distinct camera trajectories. In this paper, we propose WorldStereo, a novel framework that bridges camera-guided video generation and 3D reconstruction via two dedicated geometric memory modules. Formally, the global-geometric memory enables precise camera control while injecting coarse structural priors through incrementally updated point clouds. Moreover, the spatial-stereo memory constrains the model's attention receptive fields with 3D correspondence to focus on fine-grained details from the memory bank. These components enable WorldStereo to generate multi-view-consistent videos under precise camera control, facilitating high-quality 3D reconstruction. Furthermore, the flexible control branch-based WorldStereo shows impressive efficiency, benefiting from the distribution matching distilled VDM backbone without joint training. Extensive experiments across both camera-guided video generation and 3D reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Notably, we show that WorldStereo acts as a powerful world model, tackling diverse scene generation tasks (whether starting from perspective or panoramic images) with high-fidelity 3D results. Models will be released.

</details>


### [230] [FluxMem: Adaptive Hierarchical Memory for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02096)
*Yiweng Xie,Bo He,Junke Wang,Xiangyu Zheng,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FluxMem是一个无需训练的高效流式视频理解框架，通过自适应压缩冗余视觉记忆，在保持性能的同时显著降低计算和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需要实时处理连续视频流，但现有方法存在计算和内存开销大的问题。视频中存在大量时空冗余信息，需要高效压缩机制来减少资源消耗。

Method: 采用分层两阶段设计：1) 时间邻接选择(TAS)模块去除相邻帧间的冗余视觉标记；2) 空间域整合(SDC)模块合并每帧内空间重复区域。引入自适应标记压缩机制，基于场景统计自动确定压缩率。

Result: 在StreamingBench上达到76.4，OVO-Bench上达到67.2（实时设置），OVO-Bench延迟降低69.9%，峰值GPU内存减少34.5%。离线性能方面，MLVU达到73.1，视觉标记减少65%。

Conclusion: FluxMem通过自适应压缩冗余视觉记忆，在流式视频理解任务中实现了最先进的性能，同时显著降低了计算和内存开销，为高效视频分析提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents FluxMem, a training-free framework for efficient streaming video understanding. FluxMem adaptively compresses redundant visual memory through a hierarchical, two-stage design: (1) a Temporal Adjacency Selection (TAS) module removes redundant visual tokens across adjacent frames, and (2) a Spatial Domain Consolidation (SDC) module further merges spatially repetitive regions within each frame into compact representations. To adapt effectively to dynamic scenes, we introduce a self-adaptive token compression mechanism in both TAS and SDC, which automatically determines the compression rate based on intrinsic scene statistics rather than manual tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FluxMem achieves new state-of-the-art results on existing online video benchmarks, reaching 76.4 on StreamingBench and 67.2 on OVO-Bench under real-time settings, while reducing latency by 69.9% and peak GPU memory by 34.5% on OVO-Bench. Furthermore, it maintains strong offline performance, achieving 73.1 on MLVU while using 65% fewer visual tokens.

</details>


### [231] [A 3D mesh convolution-based autoencoder for geometry compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02125)
*Germain Bregeon,Marius Preda,Radu Ispas,Titus Zaharia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于3D网格卷积的自编码器用于几何压缩，无需预处理或流形/水密性条件，直接从网格面学习特征，通过专用池化和反池化保持连接性，在重建和分类任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D网格压缩方法通常需要预处理或对网格拓扑有严格要求（如流形/水密性），限制了在非规则网格数据上的应用。需要一种能够直接处理不规则网格数据且无需额外条件的压缩方法。

Method: 基于3D网格卷积的自编码器架构：编码器通过卷积层从网格面直接学习特征，使用专用池化操作压缩网格到紧凑的基础网格空间；解码器通过反池化恢复原始连接性并重建几何。该方法不依赖预处理，能处理任意拓扑的网格。

Result: 在多类数据集上的实验表明，该方法在3D网格几何重建精度和潜在空间分类任务上均优于现有最先进方法，证明了其在处理不规则网格数据时的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 提出的3D网格卷积自编码器为几何压缩提供了一种有效解决方案，能够直接处理不规则网格数据而无需预处理或拓扑限制，在重建和分类任务中表现出色，代码已开源。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel 3D mesh convolution-based autoencoder for geometry compression, able to deal with irregular mesh data without requiring neither preprocessing nor manifold/watertightness conditions. The proposed approach extracts meaningful latent representations by learning features directly from the mesh faces, while preserving connectivity through dedicated pooling and unpooling operations. The encoder compresses the input mesh into a compact base mesh space, which ensures that the latent space remains comparable. The decoder reconstructs the original connectivity and restores the compressed geometry to its full resolution. Extensive experiments on multi-class datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in both 3D mesh geometry reconstruction and latent space classification tasks. Code available at: github.com/germainGB/MeshConv3D

</details>


### [232] [LiftAvatar: Kinematic-Space Completion for Expression-Controlled 3D Gaussian Avatar Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02129)
*Hualiang Wei,Shunran Jia,Jialun Liu,Wenhui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiftAvatar是一种通过提升稀疏单目观测到运动学空间来增强3D虚拟化身动画质量的新范式，使用视频扩散Transformer合成高质量表情序列


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于3D高斯泼溅的虚拟化身由于日常单目视频中稀疏运动学线索导致的表达受限和重建伪影问题

Method: 1) 多粒度表情控制方案：结合着色图和表情系数实现精确稳定驱动；2) 多参考条件机制：聚合多帧互补线索确保3D一致性和可控性；3) 基于视频扩散Transformer合成高质量时序一致表情序列

Result: LiftAvatar作为即插即用增强器，显著提升最先进3D虚拟化身方法的动画质量和定量指标，特别是在极端未见表情条件下表现优异

Conclusion: 通过将不完整观测扩展为多样姿态-表情变化，LiftAvatar实现了从大规模视频生成模型到3D管线的有效先验蒸馏，为稀疏单目视频驱动的3D虚拟化身提供了强大的增强解决方案

Abstract: We present LiftAvatar, a new paradigm that completes sparse monocular observations in kinematic space (e.g., facial expressions and head pose) and uses the completed signals to drive high-fidelity avatar animation. LiftAvatar is a fine-grained, expression-controllable large-scale video diffusion Transformer that synthesizes high-quality, temporally coherent expression sequences conditioned on single or multiple reference images. The key idea is to lift incomplete input data into a richer kinematic representation, thereby strengthening both reconstruction and animation in downstream 3D avatar pipelines. To this end, we introduce (i) a multi-granularity expression control scheme that combines shading maps with expression coefficients for precise and stable driving, and (ii) a multi-reference conditioning mechanism that aggregates complementary cues from multiple frames, enabling strong 3D consistency and controllability. As a plug-and-play enhancer, LiftAvatar directly addresses the limited expressiveness and reconstruction artifacts of 3D Gaussian Splatting-based avatars caused by sparse kinematic cues in everyday monocular videos. By expanding incomplete observations into diverse pose-expression variations, LiftAvatar also enables effective prior distillation from large-scale video generative models into 3D pipelines, leading to substantial gains. Extensive experiments show that LiftAvatar consistently boosts animation quality and quantitative metrics of state-of-the-art 3D avatar methods, especially under extreme, unseen expressions.

</details>


### [233] [Stereo-Inertial Poser: Towards Metric-Accurate Shape-Aware Motion Capture Using Sparse IMUs and a Single Stereo Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02130)
*Tutian Tang,Xingyu Ji,Yutong Li,MingHao Liu,Wenqiang Xu,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stereo-Inertial Poser：基于单目立体相机和6个IMU的实时运动捕捉系统，解决单目深度模糊和忽略人体测量变化的问题，实现度量准确、形状感知的3D人体运动估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-惯性运动捕捉系统虽然结合单目相机和稀疏IMU解决了遮挡和漂移问题，但仍存在两个主要限制：1）单目深度模糊导致的全局平移度量不准确；2）忽略人体测量变化的形状无关局部运动估计。

Method: 使用单目立体相机替代单目RGB相机，通过校准的基线几何解决深度模糊，实现直接3D关键点提取和身体形状参数估计。融合IMU数据和视觉线索预测漂移补偿的关节位置和根运动，并引入新颖的形状感知融合模块动态协调人体测量变化与全局平移。采用端到端管道，无需基于优化的后处理。

Result: 系统达到超过200 FPS的实时性能，定量评估在多个数据集上展示最先进性能，定性结果显示在长时间录制下产生无漂移的全局平移，并减少脚部滑动效应。

Conclusion: Stereo-Inertial Poser通过立体视觉和IMU的融合，成功解决了现有系统的深度模糊和形状无关问题，实现了度量准确、形状感知的实时3D人体运动捕捉，为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in visual-inertial motion capture systems have demonstrated the potential of combining monocular cameras with sparse inertial measurement units (IMUs) as cost-effective solutions, which effectively mitigate occlusion and drift issues inherent in single-modality systems. However, they are still limited by metric inaccuracies in global translations stemming from monocular depth ambiguity, and shape-agnostic local motion estimations that ignore anthropometric variations. We present Stereo-Inertial Poser, a real-time motion capture system that leverages a single stereo camera and six IMUs to estimate metric-accurate and shape-aware 3D human motion. By replacing the monocular RGB with stereo vision, our system resolves depth ambiguity through calibrated baseline geometry, enabling direct 3D keypoint extraction and body shape parameter estimation. IMU data and visual cues are fused for predicting drift-compensated joint positions and root movements, while a novel shape-aware fusion module dynamically harmonizes anthropometry variations with global translations. Our end-to-end pipeline achieves over 200 FPS without optimization-based post-processing, enabling real-time deployment. Quantitative evaluations across various datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance. Qualitative results show our method produces drift-free global translation under a long recording time and reduces foot-skating effects.

</details>


### [234] [SimRecon: SimReady Compositional Scene Reconstruction from Real Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02133)
*Chong Xia,Kai Zhu,Zizhuo Wang,Fangfu Liu,Zhizheng Zhang,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimRecon框架通过"感知-生成-模拟"流程实现组合式场景重建，引入主动视角优化和场景图合成器解决视觉保真度和物理合理性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统组合式重建方法主要关注视觉外观，在真实场景中泛化能力有限。需要一种能够处理杂乱场景、同时保证视觉保真度和物理合理性的重建方法。

Method: 提出SimRecon框架，包含三个阶段：1) 从视频输入进行场景级语义重建；2) 执行单物体生成；3) 在模拟器中组装资产。为克服简单组合导致的问题，引入两个桥接模块：主动视角优化（优化投影图像作为单物体补全条件）和场景图合成器（指导3D模拟器中的构造过程）。

Result: 在ScanNet数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法优于先前最先进方法，能够实现更高质量的视觉保真度和物理合理性。

Conclusion: SimRecon通过感知-生成-模拟流程和两个桥接模块，有效解决了组合式场景重建中的视觉保真度和物理合理性挑战，为真实世界杂乱场景的重建提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Compositional scene reconstruction seeks to create object-centric representations rather than holistic scenes from real-world videos, which is natively applicable for simulation and interaction. Conventional compositional reconstruction approaches primarily emphasize on visual appearance and show limited generalization ability to real-world scenarios. In this paper, we propose SimRecon, a framework that realizes a "Perception-Generation-Simulation" pipeline towards cluttered scene reconstruction, which first conducts scene-level semantic reconstruction from video input, then performs single-object generation, and finally assembles these assets in the simulator. However, naively combining these three stages leads to visual infidelity of generated assets and physical implausibility of the final scene, a problem particularly severe for complex scenes. Thus, we further propose two bridging modules between the three stages to address this problem. To be specific, for the transition from Perception to Generation, critical for visual fidelity, we introduce Active Viewpoint Optimization, which actively searches in 3D space to acquire optimal projected images as conditions for single-object completion. Moreover, for the transition from Generation to Simulation, essential for physical plausibility, we propose a Scene Graph Synthesizer, which guides the construction from scratch in 3D simulators, mirroring the native, constructive principle of the real world. Extensive experiments on the ScanNet dataset validate our method's superior performance over previous state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [235] [OnlineX: Unified Online 3D Reconstruction and Understanding with Active-to-Stable State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02134)
*Chong Xia,Fangfu Liu,Yule Wang,Yize Pang,Yueqi Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OnlineX：首个在线3D高斯泼溅框架，通过解耦主动-稳定状态演化解决累积漂移问题，实现实时视觉外观和语言场重建


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可泛化3DGS方法主要遵循离线重建范式，缺乏连续重建能力，限制了其在机器人、VR/AR等在线场景中的应用。在线重建面临累积漂移的核心挑战，源于内存状态在捕获高频局部几何（主动角色）与保持长期全局结构（稳定角色）之间的根本冲突。

Method: 提出解耦主动-稳定状态演化范式：将内存状态分离为专用主动状态和持久稳定状态，然后将前者信息融合到后者以实现保真度和稳定性。联合建模视觉外观和语言场，并引入隐式高斯融合模块提升重建质量。仅使用流式图像进行在线重建。

Result: 在主流数据集上，方法在新视角合成和语义理解方面持续优于先前工作，在不同长度输入序列上展现稳健性能，并实现实时推理速度。

Conclusion: OnlineX是首个实现在线3D高斯泼溅重建的框架，通过解耦主动-稳定状态演化有效解决累积漂移问题，为机器人、VR/AR等在线应用场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled rapid 3D scene reconstruction within seconds, eliminating the need for per-scene optimization. However, existing methods primarily follow an offline reconstruction paradigm, lacking the capacity for continuous reconstruction, which limits their applicability to online scenarios such as robotics and VR/AR. In this paper, we introduce OnlineX, a feed-forward framework that reconstructs both 3D visual appearance and language fields in an online manner using only streaming images. A key challenge in online formulation is the cumulative drift issue, which is rooted in the fundamental conflict between two opposing roles of the memory state: an active role that constantly refreshes to capture high-frequency local geometry, and a stable role that conservatively accumulates and preserves the long-term global structure. To address this, we introduce a decoupled active-to-stable state evolution paradigm. Our framework decouples the memory state into a dedicated active state and a persistent stable state, and then cohesively fuses the information from the former into the latter to achieve both fidelity and stability. Moreover, we jointly model visual appearance and language fields and incorporate an implicit Gaussian fusion module to enhance reconstruction quality. Experiments on mainstream datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms prior work in novel view synthesis and semantic understanding, showcasing robust performance across input sequences of varying lengths with real-time inference speed.

</details>


### [236] [Bridging the gap between Performance and Interpretability: An Explainable Disentangled Multimodal Framework for Cancer Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02162)
*Aniek Eijpe,Soufyan Lakbir,Melis Erdal Cesur,Sara P. Oliveira,Angelos Chatzimparmpas,Sanne Abeln,Wilson Silva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DIMAFx是一个可解释的多模态癌症生存预测框架，使用组织病理学全切片图像和转录组学数据，通过解耦的模态特异性和模态共享表示实现高性能且可解释的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态生存预测模型虽然准确性提高，但复杂性降低了可解释性，限制了理解不同数据源如何影响预测的能力。需要克服性能与可解释性之间的传统权衡。

Method: 提出DIMAFx框架，从组织病理学全切片图像和转录组学数据中生成解耦的、可解释的模态特异性和模态共享表示。利用SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）进行系统解释。

Result: 在多个癌症队列中实现最先进的性能和改进的表征解耦。在乳腺癌生存预测中，最具预测性的特征包含模态共享信息，捕捉实体肿瘤形态学（主要与晚期雌激素反应相关），其中更高级别的形态学与通路上调和风险增加一致。模态特异性特征捕捉来自脂肪和基质形态学的微环境信号。

Conclusion: 多模态模型可以克服性能与可解释性之间的传统权衡，支持其在精准医学中的应用。DIMAFx展示了如何通过解耦表示和系统解释来获得高性能且可解释的预测模型。

Abstract: While multimodal survival prediction models are increasingly more accurate, their complexity often reduces interpretability, limiting insight into how different data sources influence predictions. To address this, we introduce DIMAFx, an explainable multimodal framework for cancer survival prediction that produces disentangled, interpretable modality-specific and modality-shared representations from histopathology whole-slide images and transcriptomics data. Across multiple cancer cohorts, DIMAFx achieves state-of-the-art performance and improved representation disentanglement. Leveraging its interpretable design and SHapley Additive exPlanations, DIMAFx systematically reveals key multimodal interactions and the biological information encoded in the disentangled representations. In breast cancer survival prediction, the most predictive features contain modality-shared information, including one capturing solid tumor morphology contextualized primarily by late estrogen response, where higher-grade morphology aligned with pathway upregulation and increased risk, consistent with known breast cancer biology. Key modality-specific features capture microenvironmental signals from interacting adipose and stromal morphologies. These results show that multimodal models can overcome the traditional trade-off between performance and explainability, supporting their application in precision medicine.

</details>


### [237] [GeoDiT: Point-Conditioned Diffusion Transformer for Satellite Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02172)
*Srikumar Sastry,Dan Cher,Brian Wei,Aayush Dhakal,Subash Khanal,Dev Gupta,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoDiT：一种用于文本到卫星图像生成的点控扩散变换器，通过点位置和文本描述提供语义丰富的控制，无需像素级地图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受控卫星图像生成模型通常需要耗时获取且语义有限的像素级地图，需要更灵活、标注友好的控制方法

Method: 提出基于点的条件框架，通过点空间位置和关联文本描述控制生成；引入自适应局部注意力机制，基于输入点查询正则化注意力分数；系统评估卫星图像表示对齐和地理位置表示条件等特定领域设计选择

Result: GeoDiT在生成性能上表现出色，超越了最先进的遥感生成模型

Conclusion: GeoDiT提供了一种灵活、标注友好且计算简单的卫星图像生成方法，通过点基控制实现了语义丰富的生成控制

Abstract: We introduce GeoDiT, a diffusion transformer designed for text-to-satellite image generation with point-based control. Existing controlled satellite image generative models often require pixel-level maps that are time-consuming to acquire, yet semantically limited. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel point-based conditioning framework that controls the generation process through the spatial location of the points and the textual description associated with each point, providing semantically rich control signals. This approach enables flexible, annotation-friendly, and computationally simple inference for satellite image generation. To this end, we introduce an adaptive local attention mechanism that effectively regularizes the attention scores based on the input point queries. We systematically evaluate various domain-specific design choices for training GeoDiT, including the selection of satellite image representation for alignment and geolocation representation for conditioning. Our experiments demonstrate that GeoDiT achieves impressive generation performance, surpassing the state-of-the-art remote sensing generative models.

</details>


### [238] [Kiwi-Edit: Versatile Video Editing via Instruction and Reference Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02175)
*Yiqi Lin,Guoqiang Liang,Ziyun Zeng,Zechen Bai,Yanzhe Chen,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Kiwi-Edit框架，通过可扩展的数据生成管道创建RefVIE数据集，结合可学习查询和潜在视觉特征实现基于指令和参考图像的精确视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于指令的视频编辑方法在精确视觉控制方面存在局限，因为自然语言难以描述复杂的视觉细节。虽然参考图像引导编辑提供了解决方案，但高质量配对训练数据的稀缺限制了其潜力。

Method: 1) 提出可扩展的数据生成管道，利用图像生成模型将现有视频编辑对转换为高质量训练四元组；2) 构建RefVIE大规模数据集和RefVIE-Bench评估基准；3) 设计Kiwi-Edit统一架构，结合可学习查询和潜在视觉特征进行参考语义引导；4) 采用渐进式多阶段训练课程。

Result: 模型在指令遵循和参考保真度方面取得显著提升，在可控视频编辑任务中建立了新的最先进水平。所有数据集、模型和代码均已开源。

Conclusion: 通过数据生成管道和统一架构的协同设计，解决了参考图像引导视频编辑中的数据稀缺问题，实现了更精确的视觉控制，为可控视频编辑领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Instruction-based video editing has witnessed rapid progress, yet current methods often struggle with precise visual control, as natural language is inherently limited in describing complex visual nuances. Although reference-guided editing offers a robust solution, its potential is currently bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality paired training data. To bridge this gap, we introduce a scalable data generation pipeline that transforms existing video editing pairs into high-fidelity training quadruplets, leveraging image generative models to create synthesized reference scaffolds. Using this pipeline, we construct RefVIE, a large-scale dataset tailored for instruction-reference-following tasks, and establish RefVIE-Bench for comprehensive evaluation. Furthermore, we propose a unified editing architecture, Kiwi-Edit, that synergizes learnable queries and latent visual features for reference semantic guidance. Our model achieves significant gains in instruction following and reference fidelity via a progressive multi-stage training curriculum. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our data and architecture establish a new state-of-the-art in controllable video editing. All datasets, models, and code is released at https://github.com/showlab/Kiwi-Edit.

</details>


### [239] [HiFi-Inpaint: Towards High-Fidelity Reference-Based Inpainting for Generating Detail-Preserving Human-Product Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02210)
*Yichen Liu,Donghao Zhou,Jie Wang,Xin Gao,Guisheng Liu,Jiatong Li,Quanwei Zhang,Qiang Lyu,Lanqing Guo,Shilei Wen,Weiqiang Wang,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiFi-Inpaint是一个用于生成人-产品图像的高保真参考修复框架，通过共享增强注意力和细节感知损失解决产品细节保留问题，并在新构建的HP-Image-40K数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-产品图像在广告、电商和数字营销中至关重要，但现有参考修复方法在三个方面存在局限：缺乏多样化大规模训练数据、模型难以专注产品细节保留、粗粒度监督无法实现精确引导。

Method: 提出HiFi-Inpaint框架，包含：1) 共享增强注意力(SEA)模块，用于精炼细粒度产品特征；2) 细节感知损失(DAL)，利用高频图进行像素级精确监督；3) 构建HP-Image-40K数据集，包含自合成数据和自动过滤处理。

Result: 实验结果表明HiFi-Inpaint实现了最先进的性能，能够生成细节保留良好的人-产品图像。

Conclusion: HiFi-Inpaint通过创新的注意力机制和损失函数设计，有效解决了参考修复中人-产品图像生成的产品细节保留问题，为相关应用提供了高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Human-product images, which showcase the integration of humans and products, play a vital role in advertising, e-commerce, and digital marketing. The essential challenge of generating such images lies in ensuring the high-fidelity preservation of product details. Among existing paradigms, reference-based inpainting offers a targeted solution by leveraging product reference images to guide the inpainting process. However, limitations remain in three key aspects: the lack of diverse large-scale training data, the struggle of current models to focus on product detail preservation, and the inability of coarse supervision for achieving precise guidance. To address these issues, we propose HiFi-Inpaint, a novel high-fidelity reference-based inpainting framework tailored for generating human-product images. HiFi-Inpaint introduces Shared Enhancement Attention (SEA) to refine fine-grained product features and Detail-Aware Loss (DAL) to enforce precise pixel-level supervision using high-frequency maps. Additionally, we construct a new dataset, HP-Image-40K, with samples curated from self-synthesis data and processed with automatic filtering. Experimental results show that HiFi-Inpaint achieves state-of-the-art performance, delivering detail-preserving human-product images.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [The Hidden Costs of Domain Fine-Tuning: Pii-Bearing Data Degrades Safety and Increases Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00061)
*Jayesh Choudhari,Piyush Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 领域微调会显著降低语言模型的安全拒绝能力，增加有害合规和隐私泄露风险，特别是在微调数据包含PII时


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究领域微调对安全对齐行为和隐私的影响，特别是在客户支持助手部署场景中，模型会同时处理良性领域内请求和情感、哲学或对抗性的领域外查询

Method: 对开源聊天模型（最大8B参数）进行受控实证研究，使用5,000个真实预订支持消息对进行微调，设置三种条件：无PII无角色交换、有PII无角色交换、有PII有角色交换，使用SORRY-Bench评估安全性，使用哲学问题集评估领域外行为

Result: 领域微调导致高质量拒绝率大幅下降（从42.6%降至个位数），有害合规增加；当微调数据包含PII时，模型还会在无关上下文中泄露敏感标识符；角色交换部分缓解PII泄露，但无法可靠恢复拒绝行为

Conclusion: 领域微调会严重削弱模型的安全对齐特性，特别是在数据包含PII时风险更高；需要更谨慎的微调策略来平衡领域适应性与安全隐私保护

Abstract: Domain fine-tuning is a common path to deploy small instruction-tuned language models as customer-support assistants, yet its effects on safety-aligned behavior and privacy are not well understood. In real deployments, such assistants receive a mixture of benign in-domain requests and out-of-domain user queries that are emotional, philosophical, or adversarial. Even when the target domain is benign, specialization may shift model behavior in ways that weaken refusal, increase harmful compliance, and induce privacy leakage.
  We present a controlled empirical study of how training data composition (presence vs.\ removal of PII) and fine-tuning configuration (role-swapping (RS)) shape safety and out-of-domain behavior in open-source chat models up to 8B parameters. We fine-tune each model on 5{,}000 real booking-support message pairs under three settings: \textsc{NoPII-NoRS}, \textsc{PII-NoRS}, and \textsc{PII-RS} (role-swapped). We evaluate safety using \textsc{SORRY-Bench}~\cite{xie2024sorry} adversarial prompts and assess out-of-domain behavior using a suite of philosophical questions~\cite{betley2025emergent}.
  Across models, domain fine-tuning causes a large distributional shift from high-quality refusals toward harmful compliance on \textsc{SORRY-Bench}, with the most severe degradation when PII is present in the fine-tuning data. For example, macro-averaged strong refusal drops from $42.6\%$ in base models to single digits after fine-tuning, while PII-bearing runs additionally exhibit double-digit rates of harmful responses with PII leakage. On philosophical queries, fine-tuned models frequently exhibit domain anchoring and, when trained with PII, leak sensitive identifiers in irrelevant contexts. Role-swapping partially mitigates PII leakage but does not reliably restore refusal behavior.

</details>


### [241] [Reverse CAPTCHA: Evaluating LLM Susceptibility to Invisible Unicode Instruction Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00164)
*Marcus Graves*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Reverse CAPTCHA评估框架，测试LLM是否能遵循嵌入正常文本中的不可见Unicode指令，发现工具使用显著增强模型合规性，不同模型提供商对编码方案有偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAPTCHA用于区分人类与机器，而本研究利用LLM能感知人类看不见的Unicode控制字符这一能力差距，探索通过不可见Unicode载荷进行提示注入的攻击面。

Method: 评估五个来自两个提供商的模型，使用两种编码方案（零宽度二进制和Unicode标签），四个提示级别，两种载荷框架，并启用或禁用工具使用，共收集8,308个模型输出进行分析。

Result: 工具使用显著增强合规性（Cohen's h高达1.37）；OpenAI模型偏好解码零宽度二进制，Anthropic模型偏好Unicode标签；明确解码指令可将合规性提高95个百分点；所有成对模型差异均具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了通过不可见Unicode载荷进行提示注入的未充分探索的攻击面，表明LLM对不可见指令的敏感性构成安全风险。

Abstract: We introduce Reverse CAPTCHA, an evaluation framework that tests whether large language models follow invisible Unicode-encoded instructions embedded in otherwise normal-looking text. Unlike traditional CAPTCHAs that distinguish humans from machines, our benchmark exploits a capability gap: models can perceive Unicode control characters that are invisible to human readers. We evaluate five models from two providers across two encoding schemes (zero-width binary and Unicode Tags), four hint levels, two payload framings, and with tool use enabled or disabled. Across 8,308 model outputs, we find that tool use dramatically amplifies compliance (Cohen's h up to 1.37, a large effect), that models exhibit provider-specific encoding preferences (OpenAI models decode zero-width binary; Anthropic models prefer Unicode Tags), and that explicit decoding instructions increase compliance by up to 95 percentage points within a single model and encoding. All pairwise model differences are statistically significant (p < 0.05, Bonferroni-corrected). These results highlight an underexplored attack surface for prompt injection via invisible Unicode payloads.

</details>


### [242] [Hidden in the Metadata: Stealth Poisoning Attacks on Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00172)
*Kennedy Edemacu,Mohammad Mahdi Shokri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MM-MEPA是一种针对多模态检索增强生成系统的元数据投毒攻击，仅操纵图像-文本条目的元数据而不修改视觉内容，就能影响检索结果并诱导攻击者期望的模型响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成系统虽然能增强多模态大语言模型的事实性并减少幻觉，但引入外部知识库也带来了新的攻击面。攻击者可以通过注入恶意多模态内容来影响检索和生成过程，而现有研究对这种元数据层面的攻击关注不足。

Method: 提出MM-MEPA多模态投毒攻击，专注于操纵图像-文本条目的元数据组件，而不改变相关的视觉内容。通过仅修改元数据，攻击能够引导多模态检索过程，并诱导下游生成模型产生攻击者期望的响应。

Result: 攻击在多个基准设置中表现出严重性：攻击成功率高达91%，能够持续干扰四种检索器和两种多模态生成器的系统行为。评估代表性防御策略发现，它们对这种仅针对元数据的投毒攻击基本无效。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了多模态检索增强生成系统中的一个关键漏洞，强调了开发更鲁棒、具备防御意识的检索和知识集成方法的紧迫性。元数据层面的攻击威胁需要得到更多关注。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing multimodal large language models by grounding their responses in external, factual knowledge and thus mitigating hallucinations. However, the integration of externally sourced knowledge bases introduces a critical attack surface. Adversaries can inject malicious multimodal content capable of influencing both retrieval and downstream generation. In this work, we present MM-MEPA, a multimodal poisoning attack that targets the metadata components of image-text entries while leaving the associated visual content unaltered. By only manipulating the metadata, MM-MEPA can still steer multimodal retrieval and induce attacker-desired model responses. We evaluate the attack across multiple benchmark settings and demonstrate its severity. MM-MEPA achieves an attack success rate of up to 91\% consistently disrupting system behaviors across four retrievers and two multimodal generators. Additionally, we assess representative defense strategies and find them largely ineffective against this form of metadata-only poisoning. Our findings expose a critical vulnerability in multimodal RAG and underscore the urgent need for more robust, defense-aware retrieval and knowledge integration methods.

</details>


### [243] [Detecting Cognitive Signatures in Typing Behavior for Non-Intrusive Authorship Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00177)
*David Condrey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于认知签名的非侵入式作者验证框架，通过分析打字时序模式区分真实创作与机械转录，保护隐私且抗攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成文本的泛滥使得基于输出的作者验证方法不可靠，需要新的验证方法。打字界面捕获的认知签名（击键时序模式）反映了真实创作的认知过程，为作者验证提供了新途径。

Method: 提出基于认知签名的非侵入式验证框架：1）利用大规模击键数据集（1.36亿事件）分析认知签名；2）定义认知负荷相关性（CLC）区分真实创作与机械转录；3）在现有写作界面中仅收集时序元数据保护隐私；4）通过证据量化限制生物特征泄露；5）分析认知签名对时序伪造攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 在给定假设下，估计具有85-95%的区分准确率。认知签名能抵抗击败运动级认证的时序伪造攻击，因为认知通道与语义内容纠缠。验证了认知签名作为隐私保护作者验证方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 将作者验证重新定义为一个人机交互问题，提供了保护隐私的替代方案，避免了侵入式监控。认知签名方法能有效区分真实创作与机械转录，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated text has intensified the need for reliable authorship verification, yet current output-based methods are increasingly unreliable. We observe that the ordinary typing interface captures rich cognitive signatures, measurable patterns in keystroke timing that reflect the planning, translating, and revising stages of genuine composition. Drawing on large-scale keystroke datasets comprising over 136 million events, we define the Cognitive Load Correlation (CLC) and show it distinguishes genuine composition from mechanical transcription. We present a non-intrusive verification framework that operates within existing writing interfaces, collecting only timing metadata to preserve privacy. Our analytical evaluation estimates 85 to 95 percent discrimination accuracy under stated assumptions, while limiting biometric leakage via evidence quantization. We analyze the adversarial robustness of cognitive signatures, showing they resist timing-forgery attacks that defeat motor-level authentication because the cognitive channel is entangled with semantic content. We conclude that reframing authorship verification as a human-computer interaction problem provides a privacy-preserving alternative to invasive surveillance.

</details>


### [244] [Privacy-Preserving Proof of Human Authorship via Zero-Knowledge Process Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00179)
*David Condrey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZK-PoP：基于零知识证明的过程认证系统，可在不泄露敏感行为生物特征数据的情况下验证人类创作过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决过程认证中的隐私悖论：验证人类创作需要收集行为生物特征数据（如击键动态、打字模式、编辑行为），但这些数据可能泄露作者的认知状态、健康状况和身份等敏感信息，构成GDPR第9条规定的敏感生物识别数据

Method: 使用零知识证明技术构建ZK-PoP系统，基于Groth16证明、算术电路、Pedersen承诺和Bulletproof范围证明，允许验证者确认：(a)顺序工作函数链计算正确；(b)行为特征向量落在人类群体分布范围内；(c)内容演化符合增量人类编辑模式，而无需了解底层行为数据、精确时间或中间内容

Result: ZK-PoP被证明具有计算零知识性、计算可靠性和会话间不可链接性。评估显示：1小时写作会话的证明生成时间小于30秒，192字节的证明可在8.2毫秒内验证，在实际隐私水平（epsilon >= 1.0）下，与非隐私基线相比准确率损失小于5%

Conclusion: ZK-PoP成功解决了过程认证中的隐私悖论，通过零知识证明技术实现了在不泄露敏感行为生物特征数据的情况下验证人类创作过程，为GDPR合规的过程认证提供了可行方案

Abstract: Process attestation verifies human authorship by collecting behavioral biometric evidence, including keystroke dynamics, typing patterns, and editing behavior, during the creative process. However, the very data needed to prove authenticity can reveal intimate details about an author's cognitive state, health conditions, and identity, constituting sensitive biometric data under GDPR Article 9. We resolve this privacy-attestation paradox using zero-knowledge proofs. We present ZK-PoP, a construction that allows a verifier to confirm that (a) sequential work function chains were computed correctly, (b) behavioral feature vectors fall within human population distributions, and (c) content evolution is consistent with incremental human editing, all without learning the underlying behavioral data, exact timing, or intermediate content. Our construction uses Groth16 proofs over arithmetic circuits with Pedersen commitments and Bulletproof range proofs. We prove that ZK-PoP is computationally zero-knowledge, computationally sound, and achieves unlinkability across sessions. Evaluation shows proof generation in under 30 seconds for a 1-hour writing session, with 192-byte proofs verifiable in 8.2 ms, while incurring less than 5% accuracy loss in simulation at practical privacy levels (epsilon >= 1.0) compared to non-private baselines.

</details>


### [245] [Your Inference Request Will Become a Black Box: Confidential Inference for Cloud-based Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00196)
*Chung-ju Huang,Huiqiang Zhao,Yuanpeng He,Lijian Li,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Peixuan Chen,Leye Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Talaria是一个保护隐私的LLM推理框架，通过分区执行敏感操作在客户端CVM中，使用ReMO协议掩蔽中间数据，实现隐私保护、模型性能保持和计算效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管LLM面临客户敏感数据（提示和响应）泄露给服务提供商的风险，现有方法无法同时保证隐私、模型性能和计算效率。

Method: 提出Talaria框架，将LLM流水线分区：权重无关的敏感操作在客户端CVM执行，权重相关计算卸载到云端GPU；使用Reversible Masked Outsourcing（ReMO）协议通过混合掩蔽技术可逆地模糊中间数据。

Result: Talaria能防御最先进的令牌推理攻击，将令牌重建准确率从97.5%以上降低到平均1.34%；作为无损机制保证输出与原模型相同，且不显著降低效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: Talaria是首个确保客户端提示和响应对云端不可访问，同时保护模型隐私、性能和效率的工作，解决了云LLM推理中的隐私保护难题。

Abstract: The increasing reliance on cloud-hosted Large Language Models (LLMs) exposes sensitive client data, such as prompts and responses, to potential privacy breaches by service providers. Existing approaches fail to ensure privacy, maintain model performance, and preserve computational efficiency simultaneously. To address this challenge, we propose Talaria, a confidential inference framework that partitions the LLM pipeline to protect client data without compromising the cloud's model intellectual property or inference quality. Talaria executes sensitive, weight-independent operations within a client-controlled Confidential Virtual Machine (CVM) while offloading weight-dependent computations to the cloud GPUs. The interaction between these environments is secured by our Reversible Masked Outsourcing (ReMO) protocol, which uses a hybrid masking technique to reversibly obscure intermediate data before outsourcing computations. Extensive evaluations show that Talaria can defend against state-of-the-art token inference attacks, reducing token reconstruction accuracy from over 97.5% to an average of 1.34%, all while being a lossless mechanism that guarantees output identical to the original model without significantly decreasing efficiency and scalability. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work that ensures clients' prompts and responses remain inaccessible to the cloud, while also preserving model privacy, performance, and efficiency.

</details>


### [246] [Challenges in Enabling Private Data Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00342)
*Yiwei Fu,Tianhao Wang,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了在差分隐私约束下进行数据估值的可行性，揭示了当前方法在隐私保护与估值效用之间的根本矛盾，并提出了隐私友好型估值方法的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据估值方法在实际应用中的普及（如数据集管理、审计和数据市场），其引发的隐私问题日益凸显。估值分数可能泄露个人数据是否被用于训练、是否具有异常影响力，或揭示专有数据集中的敏感模式。这促使研究者探索隐私保护的数据估值方法，但差分隐私要求输出对单个记录不敏感，而估值方法恰恰旨在衡量每个记录的影响，两者存在根本性矛盾。

Method: 论文首先分析了常见估值框架中导致过高敏感性的核心算法原语，解释了为什么简单的差分隐私机制会失效。然后推导出更易于隐私保护的估值程序的设计原则，并通过实证研究量化了隐私约束对代表性方法和数据集中排序保真度的退化影响。

Result: 研究揭示了当前方法在差分隐私下的局限性：朴素的私有化方法会破坏用于排序或属性价值所需的细粒度区分，特别是在异质数据集中，稀有样本会产生不成比例的影响。实证结果表明，隐私约束会显著降低估值排名的保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了现有数据估值方法在严格隐私保证下的局限性，为开发在差分隐私约束下仍保持实用性的估值方法奠定了基础。研究强调了需要在隐私保护与估值效用之间寻求新的平衡点，并提供了指导未来隐私友好型估值方法设计的原则框架。

Abstract: Data valuation methods quantify how individual training examples contribute to a model's behavior, and are increasingly used for dataset curation, auditing, and emerging data markets. As these techniques become operational, they raise serious privacy concerns: valuation scores can reveal whether a person's data was included in training, whether it was unusually influential, or what sensitive patterns exist in proprietary datasets. This motivates the study of privacy-preserving data valuation. However, privacy is fundamentally in tension with valuation utility under differential privacy (DP). DP requires outputs to be insensitive to any single record, while valuation methods are explicitly designed to measure per-record influence. As a result, naive privatization often destroys the fine-grained distinctions needed to rank or attribute value, particularly in heterogeneous datasets where rare examples exert outsized effects. In this work, we analyze the feasibility of DP-compatible data valuation. We identify the core algorithmic primitives across common valuation frameworks that induce prohibitive sensitivity, explaining why straightforward DP mechanisms fail. We further derive design principles for more privacy-amenable valuation procedures and empirically characterize how privacy constraints degrade ranking fidelity across representative methods and datasets. Our results clarify the limits of current approaches and provide a foundation for developing valuation methods that remain useful under rigorous privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [247] [ProtegoFed: Backdoor-Free Federated Instruction Tuning with Interspersed Poisoned Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00516)
*Haodong Zhao,Jinming Hu,Zhaomin Wu,Zongru Wu,Wei Du,Junyi Hou,Caibei Zhao,Zhuosheng Zhang,Bingsheng He,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ProtegoFed：首个针对联邦指令调优中分散式中毒数据的后门防御框架，通过频域梯度分析和全局二次聚类机制实现高效检测与净化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦指令调优中，中毒样本可能普遍存在于真实数据集并分散在所有客户端，现有防御机制对此类分散式中毒攻击无效，需要新的防御方案

Method: 1) 识别频域梯度作为区分中毒数据的鲁棒信号；2) 提出全局二次聚类机制，促进跨客户端协作识别中毒样本；3) 构建ProtegoFed框架，在训练过程中检测、移除并净化分散的中毒数据

Result: 在四个联邦学习数据集上，ProtegoFed能识别92.00%～100.00%的中毒样本，将攻击成功率降至接近零，同时保持主任务性能

Conclusion: ProtegoFed是首个能有效防御联邦指令调优中分散式中毒攻击的框架，通过频域分析和协作机制实现了高效的后门检测与净化

Abstract: Federated Instruction Tuning (FIT) enables collaborative instruction tuning of large language models across multiple organizations (clients) in a cross-silo setting without requiring the sharing of private instructions. Recent findings on natural backdoors and the existing training data collection method suggest that poisoned samples may be pervasive and inadvertently embedded in real-world datasets, potentially distributed across all clients, even if the clients are benign. This work systematically examine this threat in FIT, demonstrating that existing defenses are ineffective when poisoned data is interspersed among all clients. Addressing this challenge entails two major difficulties: identifying the distinctive characteristics of poisoned samples at each client and enabling collaborative defense when some clients are heavily dominated by poisoned samples. To address these difficulties, we identify gradients in the frequency domain as a robust signal to distinguish poisoned data. We further propose a global secondary clustering mechanism that facilitates collaborative identification of poisoned samples across clients. In summary, this paper introduces ProtegoFed, the first backdoor-free FIT framework that accurately detects, removes, and even purifies interspersed poisoned data across clients during the training. Experimental results on four FL datasets show that ProtegoFed identifies $92.00\% \sim 100.00\%$ of poisoned samples, reduces the attack success rate to almost zero, and maintains utility on the main task. Code is available at https://github.com/dongdongzhaoUP/ProtegoFed.

</details>


### [248] [On Best-Possible One-Time Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00544)
*Aparna Gupte,Jiahui Liu,Luowen Qian,Justin Raizes,Bhaskar Roberts,Mark Zhandry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子一次性程序（OTP）的最佳可能安全性，证明了通用最佳可能一次性编译器不存在，但通过引入可测试一次性程序编译器并构建SEQ安全OTP，实现了量子信道的最佳可能一次性安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性程序（OTP）允许用户在单个输入上评估程序而不泄露其他信息。经典OTP需要硬件假设，而量子OTP对于确定性功能是不可能的。虽然最近工作对某些随机化功能取得了进展，但基本限制和最强可达到的安全概念仍不清楚。本文旨在探索"最佳可能"的OTP，实现任何OTP构造可达到的最强一次性安全性。

Method: 1. 证明通用最佳可能一次性编译器不存在（假设存在损失加密方案）。2. 引入"可测试一次性程序"编译器子类，输出带有反射oracle的量子态。3. 提出广义单有效查询（SEQ）模拟安全概念，证明SEQ安全蕴含最佳可能可测试一次性安全。4. 在经典oracle模型中为所有量子功能构建SEQ安全OTP。5. 提出有状态量子不可区分混淆（stateful quantum iO），证明它蕴含最佳可能可测试OTP，并在经典oracle模型中可实现。

Result: 1. 证明了通用最佳可能一次性编译器不可能存在。2. 实现了最佳可能可测试OTP编译器，首次为超越经典随机化功能的任意量子信道构建OTP。3. 建立了有状态量子iO与最佳可能可测试OTP之间的蕴含关系，并在经典oracle模型中实现了有状态量子iO。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入可测试一次性程序编译器和SEQ安全概念，解决了量子OTP的最佳可能安全性问题。研究结果表明，虽然通用最佳可能一次性编译器不可能存在，但通过可测试OTP框架可以实现最强安全性。有状态量子iO被确定为实现最佳可能可测试OTP的有前景途径，为量子密码学中的程序保护提供了新方向。

Abstract: One-time programs (OTPs) aim to let a user evaluate a program on a single input while revealing nothing else. Classical OTPs require hardware assumptions, and even with quantum information, OTPs for deterministic functionalities remain impossible due to gentle-measurement attacks (Broadbent, Gutoski and Stebila, 2013). While recent works achieve positive results for certain randomized functionalities, the fundamental limits and the strongest achievable security notions remain poorly understood.
  In this paper, we ask for a "best-possible" OTP that achieves the strongest one-time security achievable by any OTP construction. We first show that a generic best-possible one-time compiler cannot exist, even for classical randomized functionalities (assuming lossy encryption schemes exist).
  Given this impossibility, we introduce a natural subclass of one-time compilers called "testable one-time program" compilers, which output quantum states augmented with reflection oracles for these program states. We show that best-possible testable OTP compilers are achievable by (1) formulating a generalized Single-Effective-Query (SEQ) simulation security notion for quantum channels and show that SEQ security implies best-possible testable one-time security, and (2) constructing SEQ-secure OTPs for all quantum functionalities in the classical oracle model. This yields the first OTP for arbitrary quantum channels beyond classical randomized functionalities.
  Finally, we propose stateful quantum indistinguishability obfuscation (stateful quantum iO) -- quantum state obfuscation for stateful quantum programs. We show that (1) stateful quantum iO implies best-possible testable OTPs and (2) stateful quantum iO is also achievable in the classical oracle model. These results identify stateful quantum iO as a promising approach towards best-possible testable OTPs.

</details>


### [249] [Where Do Smart Contract Security Analyzers Fall Short?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00890)
*Tamer Abdelaziz,Salma Alsaghir,Karim Ali*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过混合方法评估了6个主流智能合约安全分析工具在真实合约上的性能，发现准确率差异大、误报率高、分析时间长等问题，并通过开发者调查揭示了工具实际采用的主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在众多智能合约安全分析工具，但现实中的攻击仍然频繁发生，引发了对这些工具实际有效性的质疑。先前的研究主要基于合成或仅包含漏洞的合约数据集进行评估，无法全面衡量误报率、漏报率以及影响实际采用率的可用性因素。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：1）大规模基准测试：在653个真实世界智能合约上评估6个广泛使用的分析工具（Confuzzius、Dlva、Mythril、Osiris、Oyente、Slither），覆盖OWASP智能合约十大安全风险中的三个高影响漏洞类别（重入、自杀式合约终止、整数算术错误）；2）实践者调查：对150名专业开发者和审计人员进行调查，了解他们对这些工具的使用情况和认知。

Result: 结果显示：1）准确率差异显著（F1分数从31.2%到94.6%）；2）误报率高达32.6%；3）分析时间超过700秒/合约；4）开发者调查表明，过高的误报率、模糊的解释和过长的分析时间是阻碍工具信任和实际采用的主要障碍。

Conclusion: 通过将可测量的性能差距与开发者认知联系起来，该研究为提高智能合约安全分析工具的精确性、可解释性和可用性提供了具体建议，强调了减少误报、改进解释质量和优化分析时间的重要性。

Abstract: Smart contracts underpin high-value ecosystems such as decentralized finance (DeFi), yet recurring vulnerabilities continue to cause losses worth billions of dollars. Although numerous security analyzers that detect such flaws exist, real-world attacks remain frequent, raising the question of whether these tools are truly effective or simply under-used due to low developer trust. Prior benchmarks have evaluated analyzers on synthetic or vulnerable-only contract datasets, limiting their ability to measure false positives, false negatives, and usability factors that drive adoption. To close this gap, we present a mixed-methods study that combines large-scale benchmarking with practitioner insights. We evaluate six widely used analyzers (i.e., Confuzzius, Dlva, Mythril, Osiris, Oyente, and Slither) on 653 real-world smart contracts that cover three high-impact vulnerability classes from the OWASP Smart Contract Top Ten (i.e., reentrancy, suicidal contract termination, and integer arithmetic errors). Our results show substantial variation in accuracy (F1 = 31.2 to 94.6%), high false-positive rates (up to 32.6%), and runtimes exceeding 700 seconds per contract. We then survey 150 professional developers and auditors to understand how they use and perceive these tools. Our findings reveal that excessive false positives, vague explanations, and long analysis times are the main barriers to trust and adoption in practice. By linking measurable performance gaps to developer perceptions, we provide concrete recommendations for improving the precision, explainability, and usability of smart-contract security analyzers.

</details>


### [250] [Clawdrain: Exploiting Tool-Calling Chains for Stealthy Token Exhaustion in OpenClaw Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00902)
*Ben Dong,Hui Feng,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Clawdrain攻击，一种针对OpenClaw智能助手的木马化技能，通过分段验证协议实现6-9倍的令牌消耗放大，揭示了生产环境中令牌耗尽攻击的可行性及其受工具组合、恢复行为和接口设计影响的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式智能助手（如OpenClaw）的开放性和生态系统快速增长往往超出系统安全评估的速度，需要研究这些开放生态系统中的实际安全风险。

Method: 设计、实现并评估Clawdrain——一种木马化技能，通过注入SKILL.md指令和配套脚本诱导多轮"分段验证协议"，在类似生产环境的OpenClaw实例中部署，使用真实API计费和Gemini 2.5 Pro模型进行测试。

Result: Clawdrain实现了6-7倍的令牌消耗放大（相比良性基线），在失败配置下可达约9倍；观察到智能助手自主组合通用工具绕过脆弱协议步骤的现象；识别了OpenClaw架构启用的生产攻击向量。

Conclusion: 令牌耗尽攻击在实际部署中仍然可行，但其规模和可观测性受到工具组合、恢复行为和接口设计的影响；智能助手的自主工具组合能力可能改变攻击动态。

Abstract: Modern generative agents such as OpenClaw - an open-source, self-hosted personal assistant with a community skill ecosystem, are gaining attention and are used pervasively. However, the openness and rapid growth of these ecosystems often outpace systematic security evaluation. In this paper, we design, implement, and evaluate Clawdrain, a Trojanized skill that induces a multi-turn "Segmented Verification Protocol" via injected SKILL.md instructions and a companion script that returns PROGRESS/REPAIR/TERMINAL signals. We deploy Clawdrain in a production-like OpenClaw instance with real API billing and a production model (Gemini 2.5 Pro), and we measure 6-7x token amplification over a benign baseline, with a costly, failure configuration reaching approximately 9x. We observe a deployment-only phenomenon: the agent autonomously composes general-purpose tools (e.g., shell/Python) to route around brittle protocol steps, reducing amplification and altering attack dynamics. Finally, we identify production vectors enabled by OpenClaw's architecture, including SKILL.md prompt bloat, persistent tool-output pollution, cron/heartbeat frequency amplification, and behavioral instruction injection. Overall, we demonstrate that token-drain attacks remain feasible in real deployments, but their magnitude and observability are shaped by tool composition, recovery behavior, and interface design.

</details>


### [251] [AWE: Adaptive Agents for Dynamic Web Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00960)
*Akshat Singh Jaswal,Ashish Baghel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AWE是一个内存增强的多智能体框架，用于自主Web渗透测试，通过将结构化漏洞分析管道嵌入轻量级LLM编排层，显著提升注入类漏洞检测的准确性、效率和确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web应用通过AI辅助开发和快速无代码部署管道加速生产，导致软件交付速度与现有安全工具适应性之间的差距扩大。基于模式的扫描器无法推理新上下文，而新兴的LLM渗透测试器依赖无约束探索，导致高成本、不稳定行为和差的可重现性。

Method: AWE采用内存增强的多智能体框架，将结构化、漏洞特定的分析管道嵌入轻量级LLM编排层。它结合上下文感知的有效载荷突变和生成、持久内存以及浏览器支持的验证，产生确定性的、利用驱动的结果。

Result: 在104个挑战的XBOW基准测试中，AWE在注入类漏洞上取得显著提升：XSS成功率87%（比MAPTA高30.5%），盲SQL注入成功率66.7%（比MAPTA高33.3%）。尽管使用中端模型（Claude Sonnet 4）而MAPTA使用GPT-5，AWE更快、更便宜、令牌效率更高。MAPTA由于更广泛的探索能力保持更高的总体覆盖率。

Conclusion: 架构与模型推理能力同等重要：将LLM集成到有原则的、漏洞感知的管道中，能在注入类漏洞利用的准确性、效率和确定性方面带来显著提升。专业化和通用架构具有互补优势。

Abstract: Modern web applications are increasingly produced through AI-assisted development and rapid no-code deployment pipelines, widening the gap between accelerating software velocity and the limited adaptability of existing security tooling. Pattern-driven scanners fail to reason about novel contexts, while emerging LLM-based penetration testers rely on unconstrained exploration, yielding high cost, unstable behavior, and poor reproducibility.
  We introduce AWE, a memory-augmented multi-agent framework for autonomous web penetration testing that embeds structured, vulnerability-specific analysis pipelines within a lightweight LLM orchestration layer. Unlike general-purpose agents, AWE couples context aware payload mutations and generations with persistent memory and browser-backed verification to produce deterministic, exploitation-driven results.
  Evaluated on the 104-challenge XBOW benchmark, AWE achieves substantial gains on injection-class vulnerabilities - 87% XSS success (+30.5% over MAPTA) and 66.7% blind SQL injection success (+33.3%) - while being much faster, cheaper, and more token-efficient than MAPTA, despite using a midtier model (Claude Sonnet 4) versus MAPTA's GPT-5. MAPTA retains higher overall coverage due to broader exploratory capabilities, underscoring the complementary strengths of specialized and general-purpose architectures. Our results demonstrate that architecture matters as much as model reasoning capabilities: integrating LLMs into principled, vulnerability-aware pipelines yields substantial gains in accuracy, efficiency, and determinism for injection-class exploits. The source code for AWE is available at: https://github.com/stuxlabs/AWE

</details>


### [252] [BadRSSD: Backdoor Attacks on Regularized Self-Supervised Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01019)
*Jiayao Wang,Yiping Zhang,Mohammad Maruf Hasan,Xiaoying Lei,Jiale Zhang,Junwu Zhu,Qilin Wu,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BadRSSD：首个针对自监督扩散模型表示层的后门攻击方法，通过在PCA空间中劫持语义表示，控制去噪轨迹，实现高隐蔽性的目标图像生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督扩散模型的表示层存在独特安全威胁：其无约束的潜在语义空间允许隐蔽的后门攻击，使恶意控制成为可能。现有攻击主要针对生成输出，而表示层的后门攻击尚未被充分研究。

Method: 1. 在PCA空间中劫持带触发器的中毒样本的语义表示，使其指向目标图像；2. 在扩散过程中通过潜在空间、像素空间和特征分布空间的协同约束控制去噪轨迹；3. 集成表示分散正则化以保持特征空间均匀性，增强攻击隐蔽性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，BadRSSD在FID和MSE指标上显著优于现有攻击方法，能够在不同架构和配置下可靠建立后门，并能有效抵抗最先进的后门防御技术。

Conclusion: BadRSSD是首个针对自监督扩散模型表示层的后门攻击，通过多空间协同约束和表示分散正则化，实现了高实用性和高特异性的平衡，揭示了扩散模型表示层的安全脆弱性。

Abstract: Self-supervised diffusion models learn high-quality visual representations via latent space denoising. However, their representation layer poses a distinct threat: unlike traditional attacks targeting generative outputs, its unconstrained latent semantic space allows for stealthy backdoors, permitting malicious control upon triggering. In this paper, we propose BadRSSD, the first backdoor attack targeting the representation layer of self-supervised diffusion models. Specifically, it hijacks the semantic representations of poisoned samples with triggers in Principal Component Analysis (PCA) space toward those of a target image, then controls the denoising trajectory during diffusion by applying coordinated constraints across latent, pixel, and feature distribution spaces to steer the model toward generating the specified target. Additionally, we integrate representation dispersion regularization into the constraint framework to maintain feature space uniformity, significantly enhancing attack stealth. This approach preserves normal model functionality (high utility) while achieving precise target generation upon trigger activation (high specificity). Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that BadRSSD substantially outperforms existing attacks in both FID and MSE metrics, reliably establishing backdoors across different architectures and configurations, and effectively resisting state-of-the-art backdoor defenses.

</details>


### [253] [Turning Black Box into White Box: Dataset Distillation Leaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01053)
*Huajie Chen,Tianqing Zhu,Yuchen Zhong,Yang Zhang,Shang Wang,Feng He,Lefeng Zhang,Jialiang Shen,Minghao Wang,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了数据集蒸馏方法存在严重隐私泄露风险，提出信息泄露攻击(IRA)可准确预测蒸馏算法和模型架构，并能成功推断成员身份和恢复真实数据集中的敏感样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然合成数据集通常被认为是隐私保护的，但作者发现现有蒸馏方法会导致严重隐私泄露，因为合成数据集隐式编码了蒸馏模型的权重轨迹，使其变得信息过载且可被攻击者利用。

Method: 提出信息泄露攻击(IRA)，针对最先进的蒸馏技术进行攻击。该攻击能够利用合成数据集中的信息泄露来推断隐私信息。

Result: 实验表明IRA能够准确预测蒸馏算法和模型架构，并能成功推断成员身份和从真实数据集中恢复敏感样本，证明了现有蒸馏方法存在严重隐私风险。

Conclusion: 数据集蒸馏方法并非天然隐私安全，合成数据集可能编码过多信息导致隐私泄露，需要开发更安全的蒸馏技术来保护隐私。

Abstract: Dataset distillation compresses a large real dataset into a small synthetic one, enabling models trained on the synthetic data to achieve performance comparable to those trained on the real data. Although synthetic datasets are assumed to be privacy-preserving, we show that existing distillation methods can cause severe privacy leakage because synthetic datasets implicitly encode the weight trajectories of the distilled model, they become over-informative and exploitable by adversaries. To expose this risk, we introduce the Information Revelation Attack (IRA) against state-of-the-art distillation techniques. Experiments show that IRA accurately predicts both the distillation algorithm and model architecture, and can successfully infer membership and recover sensitive samples from the real dataset.

</details>


### [254] [Hide&Seek: Remove Image Watermarks with Negligible Cost via Pixel-wise Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01067)
*Huajie Chen,Tianqing Zhu,Hailin Yang,Yuchen Zhong,Yang Zhang,Hui Sun,Heng Xu,Zuobin Ying,Lihua Yin,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HIDE&SEEK (HS)是一套针对图像水印的多功能、低成本攻击方法，能够有效移除水印同时保持高视觉保真度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示最先进主动图像水印防御的局限性，当前对机器学习生成图像水印的鲁棒性研究不足

Method: 提出HIDE&SEEK (HS)攻击套件，包含多种通用且成本效益高的攻击方法

Result: 攻击能够可靠地移除嵌入的水印，同时保持高质量的视觉保真度

Conclusion: 当前SOTA图像水印防御存在可被攻击的局限性，需要更鲁棒的水印技术

Abstract: Watermarking has emerged as a key defense against the misuse of machine-generated images (MGIs). Yet the robustness of these protections remains underexplored. To reveal the limits of SOTA proactive image watermarking defenses, we propose HIDE&SEEK (HS), a suite of versatile and cost-effective attacks that reliably remove embedded watermarks while preserving high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [255] [On the Practical Feasibility of Harvest-Now, Decrypt-Later Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01091)
*Javier Blanco-Romero,Florina Almenares Mendoza,Carlos García Rubio,Celeste Campo,Daniel Díaz Sánchez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文将"现在收集，以后解密"攻击重新定义为经济问题，量化了TLS 1.2、TLS 1.3、QUIC和SSH协议中攻击者的成本，并提出了两种防御策略：存储开销和量子计算工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的发展使得"现在收集，以后解密"攻击成为现实威胁，攻击者可以存储加密通信流量，等待量子计算机能够破解密钥交换时再进行解密。论文旨在量化这种攻击的经济成本，为防御提供经济视角。

Method: 开发开源测试平台重现完整攻击序列，建立经济模型量化攻击者成本，评估TLS 1.2、TLS 1.3、QUIC和SSH协议配置策略，重点关注存储开销和量子计算工作量两个成本维度。

Result: 模型显示存储拦截流量在经济上是微不足道的，防御重点应从"能否存储"转向"解密成本"。加密客户端Hello强制攻击者进行无差别批量收集，增加存储成本；积极重新密钥和更大的密钥交换参数则增加量子计算工作量。

Conclusion: 存储成本增加对双方都有影响，而量子计算成本增加只针对攻击者，因此重新密钥和密钥大小选择是最有效的防御杠杆。这些策略为后量子密码迁移提供了深度防御，可在现有基础设施上实施。

Abstract: Harvest-now, decrypt-later (HN-DL) attacks threaten today's encrypted communications by archiving ciphertext until a quantum computer can break the underlying key exchange. This paper reframes HN-DL as an economic problem, quantifying adversary costs across Transport Layer Security (TLS) 1.2, TLS 1.3, QUIC, and Secure Shell (SSH) with an open-source testbed that reproduces the full attack sequence. Our model shows that retaining intercepted traffic is economically trivial, shifting the defensive question from whether an adversary can archive to how much decryption will cost. We evaluate protocol configuration strategies that act along two independent cost axes: storage overhead and quantum workload. Beyond the ongoing migration to post-quantum cryptography, these strategies provide defense in depth with current infrastructure. Encrypted Client Hello forces indiscriminate bulk collection, inflating the archive the adversary must retain, while aggressive rekeying and larger key exchange parameters multiply the quantum computations required to recover plaintext. Because storage inflation penalizes both sides while quantum cost inflation targets the adversary alone, rekeying and key size selection offer the strongest defensive levers.

</details>


### [256] [vEcho: A Paradigm Shift from Vulnerability Verification to Proactive Discovery with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01154)
*Mingcheng Jiang,Jiancheng Huang,Jiangfei Wang,Zhengzhu Xie,Nan Fang,Guang Cheng,Xiaoyan Hu,Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: vEcho框架将LLM从被动过滤器转变为具备学习、记忆和推理能力的虚拟安全专家，通过回响式漏洞传播机制实现从被动验证到主动发现的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SAST工具存在高误报率导致告警疲劳，现有基于LLM的方法仅将其作为被动、无状态的分类器，缺乏项目级上下文和从分析中学习以发现未知类似漏洞的能力。

Method: 提出vEcho框架：1) 将LLM转变为具备学习、记忆和推理能力的虚拟安全专家；2) 配备开发者工具套件进行深度上下文感知验证；3) 引入回响式漏洞传播机制，通过认知记忆模块模拟人类学习，从已验证漏洞中学习并主动推断未知类似缺陷。

Result: 在CWE-Bench-Java数据集上：检测率65%（相对IRIS的45.83%提升41.8%），误报率59.78%（相对IRIS的84.82%降低28.3%）。主动发现数据集外37个已知漏洞，并在开源项目中发现了51个新的0-day漏洞。

Conclusion: vEcho成功将LLM从被动过滤器转变为主动安全专家，通过回响式漏洞传播机制实现了从被动验证到主动发现的范式转变，显著提升了检测率并降低了误报率。

Abstract: Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools often suffer from high false positive rates, leading to alert fatigue that consumes valuable auditing resources. Recent efforts leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) as filters offer limited improvements; however, these methods treat LLMs as passive, stateless classifiers, which lack project-wide context and the ability to learn from analyses to discover unknown, similar vulnerabilities.In this paper, we propose vEcho, a novel framework that transforms the LLM from a passive filter into a virtual security expert capable of learning, memory, and reasoning. vEcho equips its core reasoning engine with a robust developer tool suite for deep, context-aware verification. More importantly, we introduce a novel Echoic Vulnerability Propagation (EVP) mechanism. Driven by a Cognitive Memory Module that simulates human learning, EVP enables vEcho to learn from verified vulnerabilities and proactively infer unknown, analogous flaws, achieving a paradigm shift from passive verification to active discovery.Extensive experiments on the CWE-Bench-Java dataset demonstrate vEcho's dual advantages over the state-of-the-art baseline, IRIS. Specifically, vEcho achieves a 65% detection rate, marking a 41.8% relative improvement over IRIS's 45.83%. Crucially, it simultaneously addresses alert fatigue by reducing the false positive rate to 59.78%, a 28.3% relative reduction from IRIS's 84.82%. Furthermore, vEcho proactively identified 37 additional known vulnerabilities beyond the 120 documented in the dataset, and has discovered 51 novel 0-day vulnerabilities in open-source projects.

</details>


### [257] [ATLAS: AI-Assisted Threat-to-Assertion Learning for System-on-Chip Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01170)
*Ishraq Tashdid,Kimia Tasnia,Alexander Garcia,Jonathan Valamehr,Sazadur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个LLM驱动的框架，通过结合标准化威胁建模和基于属性的形式验证，实现SoC安全自动化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SoC安全验证缺乏自动化、标准化的方法，难以将漏洞知识库（如CWE）与形式验证有效结合，需要向安全设计范式推进。

Method: 从CWE等漏洞知识库出发，识别SoC特定资产，映射相关弱点，生成基于断言的安属性和JasperGold验证脚本，结合资产中心分析和多源SoC上下文。

Result: 在三个HACK@DAC基准测试中，ATLAS检测到39/48个CWE，并为其中33个漏洞生成了正确的属性，展示了自动化知识驱动验证的有效性。

Conclusion: ATLAS通过连接标准化威胁建模和形式验证，推进了SoC安全向安全设计范式的自动化、知识驱动验证发展。

Abstract: This work presents ATLAS, an LLM-driven framework that bridges standardized threat modeling and property-based formal verification for System-on-Chip (SoC) security. Starting from vulnerability knowledge bases such as Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE), ATLAS identifies SoC-specific assets, maps relevant weaknesses, and generates assertion-based security properties and JasperGold scripts for verification. By combining asset-centric analysis with standardized threat model templates and multi-source SoC context, ATLAS automates the transformation from vulnerability reasoning to formal proof. Evaluated on three HACK@DAC benchmarks, ATLAS detected 39/48 CWEs and generated correct properties for 33 of those bugs, advancing automated, knowledge-driven SoC security verification toward a secure-by-design paradigm.

</details>


### [258] [Extending Adaptive Cruise Control with Machine Learning Intrusion Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01173)
*Lotfi Ben Othmane,Yasaswini Konapalli,Naga Prudhvi Mareedu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ACC系统通常被认为对故障注入攻击具有弹性，但研究发现卡尔曼滤波器只能容忍有界阈值内的注入值，超过阈值会导致控制器做出危险加速决策。论文提出通过集成入侵检测系统来增强PID控制器，形成ACC-IDS系统，在检测到攻击时切换到紧急制动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应巡航控制（ACC）系统通常被认为对故障注入攻击具有弹性，因为卡尔曼滤波器可以平滑噪声速度测量。然而，本研究揭示这种假设存在缺陷：卡尔曼滤波器只能容忍有界阈值内的注入值，超过阈值会导致滤波器偏离正常状态，使ACC控制器做出不安全的加速决策，可能导致碰撞。

Method: 通过分析证明和仿真结果验证卡尔曼滤波器的容忍阈值限制。主要贡献是将PID控制器与入侵检测系统（IDS）输出集成，形成ACC-IDS系统。该控制器设计简单且可实施：二进制入侵标志将控制律切换到紧急制动模式。

Result: 研究表明卡尔曼滤波器对注入速度值的容忍存在有界阈值，超过阈值会导致ACC系统失效。提出的ACC-IDS控制器在假设的检测性能和延迟约束下，能够缓解这些攻击并帮助保持ACC的碰撞避免保证。

Conclusion: 增强ACC系统以包含入侵检测功能是必要的安全措施。ACC-IDS系统通过简单的二进制切换机制，在检测到攻击时切换到紧急制动模式，能够在满足检测性能和延迟约束的条件下，有效缓解故障注入攻击并保持系统的安全保证。

Abstract: An Adaptive Cruise Control (ACC) system automatically adjusts the host vehicle's speed to maintain a safe following distance from a lead vehicle.
