In typical implementations, a feedback controller (e.g., a Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controller) computes the host vehicle's acceleration using a target speed and a spacing error, defined as the difference between the measured inter-vehicle distance and a desired safe distance. ACC is often assumed to be resilient to fault-injection attacks because a Kalman filter (KF) can smooth noisy speed measurements. However, we show--through analytical proofs and simulation results--that a KF can tolerate injected speed values only up to a bounded threshold. When injected values exceed this threshold, the filter can be driven off track, causing the ACC controller to make unsafe acceleration decisions and potentially leading to collisions. Our main contribution is to augment the PID-based controller with Intrusion Detection System (IDS) outputs, yielding Intrusion Detection Systems-Based Adaptive Cruise Control (ACC-IDS). The ACC-IDS controller is simple and implementable: a binary intrusion flag switches the control law to emergency braking. We prove that augmenting ACC with an IDS, under assumed detection-performance and latency constraints, can mitigate these attacks and help preserve ACC's collision-avoidance guarantees.

</details>


### [259] [Towards Privacy-Preserving LLM Inference via Collaborative Obfuscation (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01499)
*Yu Lin,Qizhi Zhang,Wenqiang Ruan,Daode Zhang,Jue Hong,Ye Wu,Hanning Xia,Yunlong Mao,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AloePri：首个面向工业应用、满足三项核心需求的隐私保护大语言模型推理方法，通过协变混淆保护输入输出数据，实现与明文推理相当的效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云LLM推理服务带来隐私风险，现有隐私保护方法无法同时满足工业场景的三个核心需求：最小化精度效率损失、支持异构硬件集群、兼容现有LLM基础设施

Method: 采用协变混淆技术，联合变换数据和模型参数以平衡精度和隐私；为每个模型组件精心设计变换，确保推理精度和数据隐私，同时保持与现有语言模型即服务基础设施的完全兼容

Result: 在Deepseek-V3.1-Terminus模型（671B参数）上评估显示：精度损失0.0%~3.5%，效率与明文推理相当；成功抵御最先进攻击，恢复token少于5%；首个在实际系统中适用于大规模模型的实用方法

Conclusion: AloePri是首个满足工业应用需求的隐私保护LLM推理方法，通过协变混淆技术实现了精度、效率、硬件兼容性和基础设施兼容性的平衡，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has driven the widespread adoption of cloud-based LLM inference services, while also bringing prominent privacy risks associated with the transmission and processing of private data in remote inference. For privacy-preserving LLM inference technologies to be practically applied in industrial scenarios, three core requirements must be satisfied simultaneously: (1) Accuracy and efficiency losses should be minimized to mitigate degradation in service experience. (2) The inference process can be run on large-scale clusters consist of heterogeneous legacy xPUs. (3) Compatibility with existing LLM infrastructures should be ensured to reuse their engineering optimizations. To the best of our knowledge, none of the existing privacy-preserving LLM inference methods satisfy all the above constraints while delivering meaningful privacy guarantees. In this paper, we propose AloePri, the first privacy-preserving LLM inference method for industrial applications. AloePri protects both the input and output data by covariant obfuscation, which jointly transforms data and model parameters to achieve better accuracy and privacy. We carefully design the transformation for each model component to ensure inference accuracy and data privacy while keeping full compatibility with existing infrastructures of Language Model as a Service. AloePri has been integrated into an industrial system for the evaluation of mainstream LLMs. The evaluation on Deepseek-V3.1-Terminus model (671B parameters) demonstrates that AloePri causes accuracy loss of 0.0%~3.5% and exhibits efficiency equivalent to that of plaintext inference. Meanwhile, AloePri successfully resists state-of-the-art attacks, with less than 5\% of tokens recovered. To the best of our knowledge, AloePri is the first method to exhibit practical applicability to large-scale models in real-world systems.

</details>


### [260] [DualSentinel: A Lightweight Framework for Detecting Targeted Attacks in Black-box LLM via Dual Entropy Lull Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01574)
*Xiaoyi Pang,Xuanyi Hao,Pengyu Liu,Qi Luo,Song Guo,Zhibo Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DualSentinel：一个轻量级统一防御框架，通过检测LLM生成过程中的"熵低谷"特征来准确及时识别后门和提示注入等针对性攻击的激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM防御方法通常需要高访问权限、成本高昂且会干扰正常推理，不适用于实际部署场景。针对后门和提示注入等针对性攻击会秘密迫使LLM生成特定恶意序列的问题，需要一种实用且高效的防御方案。

Method: 提出DualSentinel框架，基于"熵低谷"现象：当针对性攻击成功劫持生成过程时，LLM会表现出异常低且稳定的令牌概率熵。采用双重检查方法：1）运行时使用幅度和趋势感知监控主动敏感地标记熵低谷模式；2）触发基于任务翻转的轻量级二次验证，只有当熵低谷模式在原始任务和翻转任务中都持续存在时才确认攻击。

Result: 广泛评估表明DualSentinel高度有效（检测准确率优异，接近零误报）且效率显著（额外成本可忽略），为保护已部署LLM提供了真正实用的路径。

Conclusion: DualSentinel通过检测熵低谷模式并提供双重验证，实现了对针对性攻击的高效准确检测，解决了现有防御方法在实际部署中的局限性问题。

Abstract: Recent intelligent systems integrate powerful Large Language Models (LLMs) through APIs, but their trustworthiness may be critically undermined by targeted attacks like backdoor and prompt injection attacks, which secretly force LLMs to generate specific malicious sequences. Existing defensive approaches for such threats typically rely on high access rights, impose prohibitive costs, and hinder normal inference, rendering them impractical for real-world scenarios. To solve these limitations, we introduce DualSentinel, a lightweight and unified defense framework that can accurately and promptly detect the activation of targeted attacks alongside the LLM generation process. We first identify a characteristic of compromised LLMs, termed Entropy Lull: when a targeted attack successfully hijacks the generation process, the LLM exhibits a distinct period of abnormally low and stable token probability entropy, indicating it is following a fixed path rather than making creative choices. DualSentinel leverages this pattern by developing an innovative dual-check approach. It first employs a magnitude and trend-aware monitoring method to proactively and sensitively flag an entropy lull pattern at runtime. Upon such flagging, it triggers a lightweight yet powerful secondary verification based on task-flipping. An attack is confirmed only if the entropy lull pattern persists across both the original and the flipped task, proving that the LLM's output is coercively controlled. Extensive evaluations show that DualSentinel is both highly effective (superior detection accuracy with near-zero false positives) and remarkably efficient (negligible additional cost), offering a truly practical path toward securing deployed LLMs. The source code can be accessed at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18479273.

</details>


### [261] [Information-Theoretic Digital Twins for Stealthy Attack Detection in Industrial Control Systems: A Closed-Form KL Divergence Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01621)
*Inda Kreso,Mehran Tarif,Fatemeh Moradi,Iman Khazrak,Mostafa M Rezaee,Mohammadhossein Homaei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于信息论的闭环数字孪生框架，用于检测工业控制系统中的隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击，相比深度学习方法具有更好的精度和计算效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以检测工业控制系统中隐蔽的虚假数据注入攻击：深度学习异常检测器容易过度泛化这些细微操作，而经典故障检测方法在多变量高度相关系统中扩展性不佳

Method: 提出信息论数字孪生框架，结合N4SID系统辨识和稳态卡尔曼滤波，通过闭式KL散度量化残差分布偏移，捕捉均值偏差和恶意协方差偏移

Result: 在SWaT和WADI数据集上分别达到0.832和0.615的F1分数，精度优于TranAD等深度学习基线；计算分析显示内存需求小，推理速度比基于Transformer的方法快约600倍

Conclusion: 该框架适用于资源受限的工业边缘控制器，无需GPU加速，为实时检测隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) are increasingly used to monitor and secure Industrial Control Systems (ICS), yet detecting stealthy False Data Injection Attacks (FDIAs) that manipulate system states within normal physical bounds remains challenging. Deep learning anomaly detectors often over-generalize such subtle manipulations, while classical fault detection methods do not scale well in highly correlated multivariate systems. We propose a closed-loop Information-Theoretic Digital Twin (IT-DT) framework for real-time anomaly detection. N4SID identification is combined with steady-state Kalman filtering to quantify residual distribution shifts via closed-form KL divergence, capturing both mean deviations and malicious cross-covariance shifts. Evaluations on the SWaT and WADI datasets show that IT-DT achieves F1-scores of 0.832 and 0.615, respectively, with better precision than deep learning baselines such as TranAD. Computational profiling indicates that the analytical approach requires minimal memory and provides approximately a 600x inference speedup over transformer-based methods on CPU hardware. This makes the framework suitable for resource-constrained industrial edge controllers without GPU acceleration.

</details>


### [262] [Co-Evolutionary Multi-Modal Alignment via Structured Adversarial Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01784)
*Guoxin Shi,Haoyu Wang,Zaihui Yang,Yuxing Wang,Yongzhe Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CEMMA框架通过进化攻击者与自适应防御者的协同进化，实现多模态安全对齐，提升对抗鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法依赖静态对抗设置，在多模态场景下攻击面更大，限制了鲁棒性，需要超越静态监督的进化对齐方法

Method: 提出CEMMA框架：1) 进化攻击者将对抗提示分解为方法模板和有害意图，通过遗传算子(突变、交叉、差分进化)使简单种子攻击继承复杂越狱的结构有效性；2) 自适应防御者在合成硬负例上迭代更新，形成适应进化攻击的闭环过程

Result: 进化攻击者显著提高红队越狱攻击成功率(ASR)，自适应防御者提升跨基准的鲁棒性和泛化能力，具有更高数据效率，不引发过度良性拒绝，且与AdaShield等推理时防御兼容

Conclusion: CEMMA通过协同进化框架实现了超越静态对抗监督的多模态安全对齐，为对抗性对齐提供了自动化、自适应的方法

Abstract: Adversarial behavior plays a central role in aligning large language models with human values. However, existing alignment methods largely rely on static adversarial settings, which fundamentally limit robustness, particularly in multimodal settings with a larger attack surface. In this work, we move beyond static adversarial supervision and introduce co-evolutionary alignment with evolving attacks, instantiated by CEMMA (Co-Evolutionary Multi-Modal Alignment), an automated and adaptive framework for multimodal safety alignment. We introduce an Evolutionary Attacker that decomposes adversarial prompts into method templates and harmful intents. By employing genetic operators, including mutation, crossover, and differential evolution, it enables simple seed attacks to inherit the structural efficacy of sophisticated jailbreaks. The Adaptive Defender is iteratively updated on the synthesized hard negatives, forming a closed-loop process that adapts alignment to evolving attacks. Experiments show that the Evolutionary Attacker substantially increases red-teaming jailbreak attack success rate (ASR), while the Adaptive Defender improves robustness and generalization across benchmarks with higher data efficiency, without inducing excessive benign refusal, and remains compatible with inference-time defenses such as AdaShield.

</details>


### [263] [Phishing the Phishers with SpecularNet: Hierarchical Graph Autoencoding for Reference-Free Web Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01874)
*Tailai Song,Pedro Casas,Michela Meo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SpecularNet是一个轻量级、无参考的网页钓鱼检测框架，仅使用域名和HTML结构，通过图自编码架构实现高效检测，性能接近基于参考的方法但计算成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钓鱼检测方法存在局限性：基于参考的方法依赖外部知识库和云服务，实用性、可扩展性和可复现性受限；传统深度学习方法对不断演变的钓鱼攻击泛化能力不足。需要一种既高效又实用的解决方案。

Method: 将DOM建模为树结构，采用分层图自编码架构，结合定向、层级消息传递机制。仅使用域名和HTML结构作为输入，无需外部参考数据，在标准CPU上实现端到端快速推理。

Result: 在基准数据集上达到93.9%的F1分数，略低于最佳基于参考的方法，但推理时间从数秒减少到约20毫秒/网页。在13种SOTA检测器中表现出竞争力，计算成本显著降低。现场测试和对抗攻击评估验证了其实际部署效果。

Conclusion: 精心设计的紧凑架构可以媲美重型系统，SpecularNet证明了无参考钓鱼检测的可行性，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Phishing remains the most pervasive threat to the Web, enabling large-scale credential theft and financial fraud through deceptive webpages. While recent reference-based and generative-AI-driven phishing detectors achieve strong accuracy, their reliance on external knowledge bases, cloud services, and complex multimodal pipelines fundamentally limits practicality, scalability, and reproducibility. In contrast, conventional deep learning approaches often fail to generalize to evolving phishing campaigns. We introduce SpecularNet, a novel lightweight framework for reference-free web phishing detection that demonstrates how carefully designed compact architectures can rival heavyweight systems. SpecularNet operates solely on the domain name and HTML structure, modeling the Document Object Model (DOM) as a tree and leveraging a hierarchical graph autoencoding architecture with directional, level-wise message passing. This design captures higher-order structural invariants of phishing webpages while enabling fast, end-to-end inference on standard CPUs. Extensive evaluation against 13 state of the art phishing detectors, including leading reference-based systems, shows that SpecularNet achieves competitive detection performance with dramatically lower computational cost. On benchmark datasets, it reaches an F1 score of 93.9%, trailing the best reference-based method slightly while reducing inference time from several seconds to approximately 20 milliseconds per webpage. Field and robustness evaluations further validate SpecularNet in real-world deployments, on a newly collected 2026 open-world dataset, and against adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [264] [Systematic Survey on Privacy-Preserving Architectures for IoT and Vehicular Data Sharing: Techniques, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01876)
*Phat T. Tran-Truong,Vinh X. Q. Nguyen,Ha X. Son,Phien Nguyen-Ngoc,Khanh H. Vo,Triet M. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该调查论文系统分析了2007-2025年间75篇技术论文，提出了隐私-效率-信任三元困境，并通过三维分类法将架构分为去中心化计算、密码学基础和分布式账本三类，发现去中心化计算占主导地位，成熟方案只能在三个维度中优化一两个而牺牲其他，验证了三元困境假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和车联网系统在边缘产生大量敏感数据，需要隐私保护架构以实现安全共享而不暴露原始信息。现有解决方案面临隐私-效率-信任三元困境：同时实现强隐私保证、资源受限设备的计算效率以及去中心化信任，单一范式方法难以解决。

Method: 1. 系统分析2007-2025年间75篇技术论文；2. 提出新颖的三维分类法：去中心化计算、密码学基础和分布式账本方法；3. 进行时间分析（发现2024-2025年加速明显）；4. 全面安全威胁映射；5. 技术成熟度评估。

Result: 1. 时间分析显示2024-2025年加速显著，48%的论文在此期间发表；2. 去中心化计算占主导（44%的贡献，2025年占59%）；3. 成熟解决方案仅能在狭窄设计区域内优化一或两个维度而牺牲其他，验证了三元困境假设；4. 识别出结合互补范式的新兴混合架构是未来关键路径。

Conclusion: 现有成熟方案无法同时解决隐私-效率-信任三元困境，新兴混合架构结合互补范式是必要发展方向。未来需解决跨层安全保证组合、多层协调开销最小化和后量子安全集成等关键挑战，以实现在下一代智能交通系统和物联网生态系统中的实际部署。

Abstract: The proliferation of IoT and V2X systems generates unprecedented sensitive data at the network edge, demanding privacy-preserving architectures that enable secure sharing without exposing raw information. Contemporary solutions face a fundamental privacy-efficiency-trust trilemma: achieving strong privacy guarantees, computational efficiency for resource-constrained devices, and decentralized trust simultaneously remains intractable with single-paradigm approaches. This survey systematically analyzes 75 technical papers (2007--2025) through a novel three-dimensional taxonomy classifying architectures into Decentralized Computation, Cryptography-based, and Distributed Ledger approaches. Temporal analysis reveals dramatic acceleration during 2024--2025, with 48% of all papers published in this period -- Decentralized Computation dominates at 44% of contributions and 59% of 2025 publications. Comprehensive Security Threat Mapping and Technology Maturity Assessment demonstrate that mature solutions occupy narrow design regions excelling in one or two dimensions while compromising others, conclusively validating the trilemma hypothesis. We identify emerging hybrid architectures combining complementary paradigms as the essential path forward. Critical challenges including security guarantee composition across layers, multi-layer coordination overhead minimization, and post-quantum security integration must be addressed for practical deployment in next-generation intelligent transportation systems and IoT ecosystems.

</details>


### [265] [Real Money, Fake Models: Deceptive Model Claims in Shadow APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01919)
*Yage Zhang,Yukun Jiang,Zeyuan Chen,Michael Backes,Xinyue Shen,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统审计了官方LLM API与影子API之间的差异，发现影子API存在性能偏差、安全行为不可预测和身份验证失败等欺骗行为，严重影响了科学研究的可重复性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型（如GPT-5和Gemini-2.5）的高定价、支付障碍和区域限制催生了影子API的泛滥。这些第三方服务声称通过间接访问提供无区域限制的官方模型服务，但其输出是否与官方API一致尚不明确，这引发了对下游应用可靠性和依赖这些API的研究结果有效性的担忧。

Method: 首先识别了187篇学术论文中使用的17个影子API，然后对三个代表性影子API进行了多维度审计，涵盖效用、安全和模型验证三个方面。通过性能比较、安全行为分析和指纹测试等方法，系统评估了影子API与官方API的一致性。

Result: 研究发现：1）性能差异高达47.21%；2）安全行为存在显著不可预测性；3）45.83%的指纹测试中身份验证失败。这些发现揭示了影子API的欺骗行为，包括间接和直接的证据。

Conclusion: 影子API的欺骗行为严重破坏了科学研究的可重复性和有效性，损害了影子API用户的利益，并损害了官方模型提供商的声誉。该研究强调了需要更严格的API验证机制和透明度要求。

Abstract: Access to frontier large language models (LLMs), such as GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5, is often hindered by high pricing, payment barriers, and regional restrictions. These limitations drive the proliferation of $\textit{shadow APIs}$, third-party services that claim to provide access to official model services without regional limitations via indirect access. Despite their widespread use, it remains unclear whether shadow APIs deliver outputs consistent with those of the official APIs, raising concerns about the reliability of downstream applications and the validity of research findings that depend on them. In this paper, we present the first systematic audit between official LLM APIs and corresponding shadow APIs. We first identify 17 shadow APIs that have been utilized in 187 academic papers, with the most popular one reaching 5,966 citations and 58,639 GitHub stars by December 6, 2025. Through multidimensional auditing of three representative shadow APIs across utility, safety, and model verification, we uncover both indirect and direct evidence of deception practices in shadow APIs. Specifically, we reveal performance divergence reaching up to $47.21\%$, significant unpredictability in safety behaviors, and identity verification failures in $45.83\%$ of fingerprint tests. These deceptive practices critically undermine the reproducibility and validity of scientific research, harm the interests of shadow API users, and damage the reputation of official model providers.

</details>


### [266] [Accurate, private, secure, federated U-statistics with higher degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01986)
*Quentin Sinh,Jan Ramon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多方计算的安全联邦学习协议，用于在中心差分隐私下高效计算k≥2阶U统计量，相比现有方法显著提升精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅支持低效的本地差分隐私模型，且在处理大规模域离散化时性能不佳，需要更高效、更精确的联邦U统计量计算方法

Method: 利用多方计算技术设计安全协议，在中心差分隐私模型下计算k阶U统计量，通过理论分析优化精度、通信和计算效率

Result: 方法显著提升精度，例如对于Kendall's τ系数，均方误差比现有基线降低多达四个数量级，理论分析和实证评估均显示优越性能

Conclusion: 提出的基于多方计算的协议在中心差分隐私下高效计算U统计量，相比现有方法在精度和效率方面均有显著改进，适用于多种实用统计量计算

Abstract: We study the problem of computing a U-statistic with a kernel function f of degree k $\ge$ 2, i.e., the average of some function f over all k-tuples of instances, in a federated learning setting. Ustatistics of degree 2 include several useful statistics such as Kendall's $τ$ coefficient, the Area under the Receiver-Operator Curve and the Gini mean difference. Existing methods provide solutions only under the lower-utility local differential privacy model and/or scale poorly in the size of the domain discretization. In this work, we propose a protocol that securely computes U-statistics of degree k $\ge$ 2 under central differential privacy by leveraging Multi Party Computation (MPC). Our method substantially improves accuracy when compared to prior solutions. We provide a detailed theoretical analysis of its accuracy, communication and computational properties. We evaluate its performance empirically, obtaining favorable results, e.g., for Kendall's $τ$ coefficient, our approach reduces the Mean Squared Error by up to four orders of magnitude over existing baselines.

</details>


### [267] [Protection against Source Inference Attacks in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02017)
*Andreas Athanasiou,Kangsoo Jung,Catuscia Palamidessi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对联邦学习中源推断攻击的防御方法，通过参数级洗牌与余数系统相结合，在保持模型精度的同时将攻击准确率降至随机猜测水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽然最初被设计为隐私保护的机器学习范式，但已被证明容易受到一系列隐私攻击。最近，源推断攻击引起了关注，在这种攻击中，诚实但好奇的中心服务器试图识别训练阶段使用的特定数据点属于哪个客户端。令人担忧的是，标准的差分隐私梯度混淆技术对源推断攻击无效，至少在不过度降低准确性的情况下是如此。

Method: 论文提出在广泛研究的联邦学习洗牌模型中防御源推断攻击。首先证明标准的天真洗牌不足以防止源推断攻击。为了有效防御，需要在更细粒度级别应用洗牌；提出了一种新颖的参数级洗牌与余数系统相结合的方法。该方法提供对源推断攻击的鲁棒保护，不影响联合模型的准确性，并可无缝集成到其他隐私保护机制中。

Result: 在一系列模型和数据集上的实验证实，标准洗牌方法无法防止源推断攻击，而相比之下，提出的方法将攻击准确率降至随机猜测水平。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种有效的源推断攻击防御方法，通过参数级洗牌与余数系统的创新结合，在保持联邦学习模型性能的同时提供强大的隐私保护，解决了现有差分隐私技术在防御此类攻击时的局限性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) was initially proposed as a privacy-preserving machine learning paradigm. However, FL has been shown to be susceptible to a series of privacy attacks. Recently, there has been concern about the Source Inference Attack (SIA), where an honest-but-curious central server attempts to identify exactly which client owns a given data point which was used in the training phase. Alarmingly, standard gradient obfuscation techniques with Differential Privacy have been shown to be ineffective against SIAs, at least without severely diminishing the accuracy.
  In this work, we propose a defense against SIAs within the widely studied shuffle model of FL, where an honest shuffler acts as an intermediary between the clients and the server. First, we demonstrate that standard naive shuffling alone is insufficient to prevent SIAs. To effectively defend against SIAs, shuffling needs to be applied at a more granular level; we propose a novel combination of parameter-level shuffling with the residue number system (RNS). Our approach provides robust protection against SIAs without affecting the accuracy of the joint model and can be seamlessly integrated into other privacy protection mechanisms.
  We conduct experiments on a series of models and datasets, confirming that standard shuffling approaches fail to prevent SIAs and that, in contrast, our proposed method reduce the attack's accuracy to the level of random guessing.

</details>


### [268] [Boosting Device Utilization in Control Flow Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02161)
*Alexandra Lengert,Adam Ilyas Caulfield,Ivan De Oliveira Nunes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CARAMEL提出了一种新的控制流审计架构，通过硬件-软件协同设计的根信任机制，允许证明者应用在传输控制流证据时继续执行，显著提高了CPU利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制流审计(CFAud)架构存在"忙等待"问题，硬件根信任(RoT)需要保持执行控制以确保控制流证据传输给验证者，这严重降低了MCU的CPU利用率。

Method: CARAMEL采用硬件-软件协同设计的RoT架构，设计了具有独立专用通信接口的新RoT，允许证明者应用在证据传输期间恢复执行，避免传输延迟导致的争用。

Result: CARAMEL显著提高了CPU利用率，同时以适度的硬件成本保持了安全性，实现已开源。

Conclusion: CARAMEL通过创新的硬件-软件协同设计解决了CFAud中的CPU利用率瓶颈，为安全关键系统中的MCU提供了更高效的控制流审计方案。

Abstract: Micro-Controller Units (MCUs) are widely used in safety-critical systems, making them attractive targets for attacks. This calls for lightweight defenses that remain effective despite software compromise. Control Flow Auditing (CFAud) is one such mechanism wherein a remote verifier (Vrf) is guaranteed to received evidence about the control flow path taken on a prover (Prv) MCU, even when Prv software is compromised. Despite promising benefits, current CFAud architectures unfortunately require a ``busy-wait'' phase where a hardware-anchored root-of-trust (RoT) in Prv retains execution control to ensure delivery of control flow evidence to Vrf. This drastically reduces the CPU utilization on Prv.
  In this work, we addresses this limitation with an architecture for Contention Avoidance in Runtime Auditing with Minimized Execution Latency (CARAMEL). CARAMEL is a hardware-software RoT co-design that enables Prv applications to resume while control flow evidence is transmitted to Vrf. This significantly reduces contention due to transmission delays and improves CPU utilization without giving up on security. Key to CARAMEL is our design of a new RoT with a self-contained (and minimal) dedicated communication interface. CARAMEL's implementation and accompanying evaluation are made open-source. Our results show substantially improved CPU utilization at a modest hardware cost.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [The Partition Principle Revisited: Non-Equal Volume Designs Achieve Minimal Expected Star Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00202)
*Xiaoda Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了非等体积划分下的期望星偏差，证明了新设计的非等体积划分比经典抖动采样具有更低的期望星偏差，并推导了改进的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非等体积划分在数值积分中的应用潜力，探索比传统抖动采样更优的划分策略，为高维数值积分提供理论基础。

Method: 设计了一类新的非等体积划分方法，建立了星偏差的强划分原理，通过理论证明比较了非等体积划分采样与经典抖动采样的期望星偏差，并推导了显式上界。

Result: 证明了非等体积划分采样点集比抖动采样具有更低的期望星偏差（$\mathbb{E}(D^{*}_{N}(Z)) < \mathbb{E}(D^{*}_{N}(Y))$），推导了改进的期望星偏差上界，这些上界优于现有抖动采样的上界。

Conclusion: 新设计的非等体积划分在期望星偏差方面优于经典抖动采样，为高维数值积分中非等体积划分的应用提供了理论依据，表明这种划分策略具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We study the expected star discrepancy under a newly designed class of non-equal volume partitions. The main contributions are twofold. First, we establish a strong partition principle for the star discrepancy, showing that our newly designed non-equal volume partitions yield stratified sampling point sets with lower expected star discrepancy than classical jittered sampling. Specifically, we prove that $\mathbb{E}(D^{*}_{N}(Z)) < \mathbb{E}(D^{*}_{N}(Y))$, where $Y$ and $Z$ represent jittered sampling and our non-equal volume partition sampling, respectively. Second, we derive explicit upper bounds for the expected star discrepancy under our non-equal volume partition models, which improve upon existing bounds for jittered sampling. Our results provide a theoretical foundation for using non-equal volume partitions in high-dimensional numerical integration.

</details>


### [270] [Learning with the Nash-Sutcliffe loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00968)
*Hristos Tyralis,Georgia Papacharalampous*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文为Nash-Sutcliffe效率(NSE)建立了决策理论基础，证明其负向对应形式Nash-Sutcliffe损失(L_NS)是严格一致的，并提出了适用于多时间序列的Nash-Sutcliffe线性回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nash-Sutcliffe效率(NSE)被广泛用于多时间序列预测评估，但缺乏决策理论基础。需要为NSE在多时间序列评估中的使用提供理论依据，并扩展其应用范围。

Method: 研究NSE的负向对应形式Nash-Sutcliffe损失(L_NS = 1 - NSE)，证明L_NS对于可引出且可识别的多维函数（称为Nash-Sutcliffe函数）是严格一致的。该函数是数据加权的分量均值。提出Nash-Sutcliffe线性回归模型，通过最小化平均L_NS进行估计，简化为数据加权最小二乘公式。

Result: 证明了L_NS的严格一致性，建立了NSE的决策理论基础。提出的Nash-Sutcliffe线性回归模型能够处理具有不同随机特性的多个平稳依赖时间序列，为大数据集中的模型估计和预测评估提供了理论支持。

Conclusion: 为NSE在多时间序列预测评估中的使用建立了决策理论基础，提出的Nash-Sutcliffe线性回归模型扩展了NSE的应用范围，为大数据分析提供了更自然的实证实现，并阐明了全局机器学习模型相对于局部模型的优势。

Abstract: The Nash-Sutcliffe efficiency ($\text{NSE}$) is a widely used, positively oriented relative measure for evaluating forecasts across multiple time series. However, it lacks a decision-theoretic foundation for this purpose. To address this, we examine its negatively oriented counterpart, which we refer to as Nash-Sutcliffe loss, defined as $L_{\text{NS}} = 1 - \text{NSE}$. We prove that $L_{\text{NS}}$ is strictly consistent for an elicitable and identifiable multi-dimensional functional, which we name the Nash-Sutcliffe functional. This functional is a data-weighted component-wise mean. The common practice of maximizing the average NSE across multiple series is the sample analog of minimizing the expected $L_{\text{NS}}$. Consequently, this operation implicitly assumes that all series originate from a single non-stationary, stochastic process. We introduce Nash-Sutcliffe linear regression, a multi-dimensional model estimated by minimizing the average $L_{\text{NS}}$, which reduces to a data-weighted least squares formulation. By reorienting the sample average loss function, we extend the previously proposed evaluation and estimation framework to forecasting multiple stationary dependent time series with differing stochastic properties. This constitutes a more natural empirical implementation of the $\text{NSE}$ than the earlier formulation. Our results establish a decision-theoretic foundation for $\text{NSE}$-based model estimation and forecast evaluation in large datasets, while further clarifying the benefits of global over local machine learning models.

</details>


### [271] [Adaptive Estimation and Inference in Conditional Moment Models via the Discrepancy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01337)
*Jiyuan Tan,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于差异原则的自适应超参数选择框架，用于条件矩约束下的不适定线性逆问题，无需已知光滑度参数即可平衡偏差与方差，实现自适应估计与推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正则化估计器（如RDIV）需要先验知道光滑度参数（通常由beta源条件编码）来调整正则化参数。实践中该光滑度未知，超参数误设会导致次优收敛或不稳定。

Method: 引入基于差异原则的自适应超参数选择框架，自动平衡偏差与方差，不依赖未知光滑度参数。该框架适用于RDIV和TRAE，并在此基础上构建完全自适应的双重稳健估计器用于线性泛函。

Result: 框架在弱和强度量下达到相同收敛率。构建的双重稳健估计器在更好条件的原问题或对偶问题上达到最优率，为不适定计量模型提供实用、理论可靠的自适应推断方法。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应框架解决了不适定线性逆问题中未知光滑度参数的挑战，实现了无需先验知识的自适应超参数选择，为计量经济学模型提供了理论可靠的自适应推断工具。

Abstract: We study adaptive estimation and inference in ill-posed linear inverse problems defined by conditional moment restrictions. Existing regularized estimators such as Regularized DeepIV (RDIV) require prior knowledge of the smoothness of the nuisance function, typically encoded by a beta source condition to tune their regularization parameters. In practice, this smoothness is unknown, and misspecified hyperparameters can lead to suboptimal convergence or instability.
  We introduce a discrepancy-principle-based framework for adaptive hyperparameter selection that automatically balances bias and variance without relying on the unknown smoothness parameter. Our framework applies to both RDIV (Li et al. [2024]) and the Tikhonov Regularized Adversarial Estimator (TRAE) (Bennett et al. [2023a]) and achieves the same rates in both weak and strong metrics. Building on this, we construct a fully adaptive doubly robust estimator for linear functionals that attains the optimal rate of the better-conditioned primal or dual problem, providing a practical, theoretically grounded approach for adaptive inference in ill-posed econometric models.

</details>


### [272] [Co-optimization for Adaptive Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01719)
*Xiaoyi Su,Zhixin Zhou,Rui Luo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CoCP是一种联合优化中心点和半径的自适应共形预测框架，通过交替学习半径和中心点来生成更短、条件覆盖更好的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形回归区间在异方差和偏态分布下效率低下，特别是CQR等方法采用固定中心概念和等尾误差，导致区间偏离高密度区域且宽度不必要地增大。

Method: CoCP框架联合优化中心函数m(x)和半径函数h(x)，交替进行：(1)通过折叠绝对残差的分位数回归学习h(x)；(2)使用可微软覆盖目标优化m(x)，其梯度集中在当前边界附近。有限样本边际有效性通过分裂共形校准保证。

Result: 理论分析表明CoCP渐近逼近目标覆盖水平下的长度最小化条件区间。实验证明CoCP在合成和真实基准数据集上产生更短的区间，并达到最先进的条件覆盖诊断指标。

Conclusion: CoCP通过联合优化中心点和半径，有效解决了传统共形预测在异方差和偏态分布下的低效问题，生成了更短且条件覆盖更好的预测区间。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, but standard conformal regression intervals can be inefficient under heteroscedasticity and skewness. In particular, popular constructions such as conformalized quantile regression (CQR) often inherit a fixed notion of center and enforce equal-tailed errors, which can displace the interval away from high-density regions and produce unnecessarily wide sets. We propose Co-optimization for Adaptive Conformal Prediction (CoCP), a framework that learns prediction intervals by jointly optimizing a center $m(x)$ and a radius $h(x)$.CoCP alternates between (i) learning $h(x)$ via quantile regression on the folded absolute residual around the current center, and (ii) refining $m(x)$ with a differentiable soft-coverage objective whose gradients concentrate near the current boundaries, effectively correcting mis-centering without estimating the full conditional density. Finite-sample marginal validity is guaranteed by split-conformal calibration with a normalized nonconformity score. Theory characterizes the population fixed point of the soft objective and shows that, under standard regularity conditions, CoCP asymptotically approaches the length-minimizing conditional interval at the target coverage level as the estimation error and smoothing vanish. Experiments on synthetic and real benchmarks demonstrate that CoCP yields consistently shorter intervals and achieves state-of-the-art conditional-coverage diagnostics.

</details>


### [273] [LOCUS: A Distribution-Free Loss-Quantile Score for Risk-Aware Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01971)
*Matheus Barreto,Mário de Castro,Thiago R. Ramos,Denis Valle,Rafael Izbicki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Locus是一种分布无关的包装器，为固定预测函数生成每个输入的损失尺度可靠性分数，用于量化预测函数的实际损失而非标签不确定性，通过简单分裂校准步骤获得可跨输入比较的分数，可用于排名或阈值化以控制大损失事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型可能在平均意义上准确，但仍会产生主导部署成本的错误。需要一种方法来量化每个输入预测的可靠性，特别是预测函数可能产生的大损失事件。

Method: Locus是一个分布无关的包装器，使用任何能够输出给定输入的损失预测分布的引擎来建模预测函数的实际损失。通过简单的分裂校准步骤，将这一函数转化为分布无关、可解释的分数，该分数可跨输入比较，并能解读为损失上限。该分数可用于排名，也可通过阈值化获得具有分布无关大损失事件控制的透明标记规则。

Result: 在13个回归基准测试上的实验表明，Locus能够有效进行风险排名，并相比标准启发式方法减少大损失频率。

Conclusion: Locus提供了一种实用的方法来评估预测函数的可靠性，通过建模实际损失而非标签不确定性，为机器学习部署中的风险管理和决策提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models can be accurate on average yet still make mistakes that dominate deployment cost. We introduce Locus, a distribution-free wrapper that produces a per-input loss-scale reliability score for a fixed prediction function. Rather than quantifying uncertainty about the label, Locus models the realized loss of the prediction function using any engine that outputs a predictive distribution for the loss given an input. A simple split-calibration step turns this function into a distribution-free interpretable score that is comparable across inputs and can be read as an upper loss level. The score is useful on its own for ranking, and it can optionally be thresholded to obtain a transparent flagging rule with distribution-free control of large-loss events. Experiments across 13 regression benchmarks show that Locus yields effective risk ranking and reduces large-loss frequency compared to standard heuristics.

</details>


### [274] [Density-Matrix Spectral Embeddings for Categorical Data: Operator Structure and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01975)
*Raquel Bosch-Romeu,Antonio Falcó,osé-Antonio Rodríguez-Gallego*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于密度矩阵构造的监督降维方法，用于分类数据，通过类别条件频率诱导，在降维空间中进行核密度估计分类


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对分类数据（和离散化混合类型数据）的监督降维需求，传统方法可能无法有效处理高基数、稀疏性、噪声和类别不平衡等问题，需要一种基于类别条件频率的密度矩阵构造方法

Method: 将标记数据集编码为one-hot向量，构建频率矩阵聚合每个类别内的特征出现情况，定义归一化的Gram型算子作为密度矩阵，利用主导特征模式进行低维谱嵌入，在降维空间中使用类别条件核密度估计和最大似然决策规则进行分类

Result: 该方法具有结构不变性，复杂度可控，内在秩受类别数限制，在合成基准测试中验证了对高基数、稀疏性、噪声和类别不平衡等挑战的有效处理能力

Conclusion: 提出的基于密度矩阵的监督降维方法为分类数据提供了一种有效的低维表示，通过谱嵌入和核密度估计分类，在多种挑战性场景下表现出鲁棒性

Abstract: We introduce a supervised dimensionality reduction methodology for categorical (and discretized mixed-type) data based on a density-matrix construction induced by class-conditional frequencies. Given a labeled dataset encoded in a one-hot survey space, we assemble a frequency matrix whose columns aggregate feature occurrences within each class, and define a normalized Gram-type operator that satisfies the axioms of a density matrix. The resulting representation admits an intrinsic rank bound controlled by the number of classes, enabling low-dimensional spectral embeddings via dominant eigenmodes.
  Classification is performed in the reduced space through class-conditional kernel density estimation and a maximum-likelihood decision rule.
  We establish structural invariances, provide complexity estimates, and validate the approach on synthetic benchmarks probing high cardinality, sparsity, noise, and class imbalance.

</details>


### [275] [TRAKNN: Efficient Trajectory Aware Spatiotemporal kNN for Rare Meteorological Trajectory Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02059)
*Guillaume Coulaud,Davide Faranda*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TRAKNN：一种无监督、数据无关的框架，用于在时空数据中检测几何上罕见的短轨迹，采用精确kNN方法，可高效处理多年代、大陆尺度的网格数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气事件（如风暴和热浪）由持续数天的持久大气环流模式驱动。传统环流研究通常关注瞬时大气状态，但捕获这些空间场的时间演化（轨迹）对于表征罕见且可能具有影响的大气行为至关重要。然而，在多年代、大陆尺度的网格数据集上进行详尽的相似性搜索存在显著的计算和内存挑战。

Method: 提出TRAKNN（轨迹感知KNN）框架，这是一种完全无监督且数据无关的方法，用于在时空数据中检测几何上罕见的短轨迹。采用精确kNN方法，利用基于递归的算法将计算复杂度与轨迹长度解耦，并通过高效的批量操作最大化计算强度。这些优化使得在标准工作站（CPU或GPU）上能够进行详尽分析。

Result: 在75年欧洲日平均海平面气压数据上评估该方法。结果表明，TRAKNN识别的罕见轨迹对应于物理上一致的大气异常，并与独立的极端事件数据库相吻合。

Conclusion: TRAKNN框架能够高效地在大规模时空数据中检测罕见的轨迹模式，为极端天气事件的环流演变分析提供了有效的计算工具，验证了其在识别物理相关大气异常方面的有效性。

Abstract: Extreme weather events, such as windstorms and heatwaves, are driven by persistent atmospheric circulation patterns that evolve over several consecutive days. While traditional circulation-based studies often focus on instantaneous atmospheric states, capturing the temporal evolution, or trajectory, of these spatial fields is essential for characterizing rare and potentially impactful atmospheric behavior. However, performing an exhaustive similarity search on multi-decadal, continental-scale gridded datasets presents significant computational and memory challenges. In this paper, we propose TRAKNN (TRajectory Aware KNN), a fully unsupervised and data-agnostic framework for detecting geometrically rare short trajectories in spatio-temporal data with an exact kNN approach. TRAKNN leverages a recurrence-based algorithm that decouples computational complexity from trajectory length and efficient batch operations, maximizing computational intensity. These optimizations enable exhaustive analysis on standard workstations, either on CPU or on GPU. We evaluate our approach on 75 years of daily European sea-level pressure data. Our results illustrate that rare trajectories identified by TRAKNN correspond to physically coherent atmospheric anomalies and align with independent extreme-event databases.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [The relative efficiency of sequential tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00216)
*Henri Doerks,Erik Ekström,Yuqiong Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了序贯检验相对于固定样本量检验在样本量减少方面的效率，针对布朗运动漂移检验场景，量化了序贯概率比检验(Wald SPRT)相比固定样本量检验的平均样本量减少幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序贯方法允许根据达到特定精度所需来调整样本量，相比固定样本量方法能够减少平均观测次数。然而，这种减少的具体幅度尚未得到精确量化，需要建立理论界限来评估序贯检验相对于固定样本量检验的效率优势。

Method: 研究聚焦于检验布朗运动的未知漂移参数，比较Wald序贯概率比检验(SPRT)与使用预定固定样本量的检验方法。通过理论分析，推导出实现给定检验精度所需平均样本量减少的精确界限。

Result: 对于对称误差界限，序贯检验将平均样本量减少至少36%至多75%。样本量减少幅度随检验功效单调增加，表明对更高功效要求时，序贯检验的相对优势更大。对于非对称误差界限情况，研究提供了基于对称情况的下界。

Conclusion: 序贯检验相比固定样本量检验能显著减少所需平均样本量，且这种效率优势随检验功效要求提高而增强。研究为序贯检验的效率提供了精确的量化界限，有助于在实际应用中评估采用序贯方法的潜在收益。

Abstract: While many statistical procedures rely on a fixed sample size, sequential methods allow a decision-maker to adapt the sample size to achieve a given precision. In this way, sequential tests reduce the average number of observations required to achieve a given power of the test -- but by how much? To address this question, we focus on the scenario of testing the unknown drift of a Brownian motion, comparing the Wald sequential probability ratio test with tests that use a pre-determined fixed sample size. We provide precise bounds on the average reduction in sample size needed to achieve a desired precision. Specifically, we demonstrate that for symmetric error bounds, the sequential test reduces the average sample size by at least 36\% and by at most 75\%. Moreover, the reduction in sample size increases monotonically with the power of the test, meaning that the relative advantage of using a sequential test over a fixed sample size test grows as higher power is required. We also study the relative efficiency in the case with asymmetric error bounds, and we provide a lower bound in terms of the symmetric case.

</details>


### [277] [Minimax Simple Bayes Estimators of a Normal Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00553)
*Yuzo Maruyama*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文为Maruyama和Strawderman(2006)的后续研究，针对正态方差在熵损失下的估计问题，建立了广义贝叶斯估计量的明确闭式极小极大性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然前作(Maruyama和Strawderman, 2006)已经证明了这类简单形式的广义贝叶斯估计量的贝叶斯性，但未能提供其极小极大性的闭式结果。本文旨在填补这一空白，为该类简单贝叶斯估计量建立明确的极小极大性理论结果。

Method: 本文重新审视正态方差在熵损失下的估计问题，针对Maruyama和Strawderman(2006)提出的具有简单形式的广义贝叶斯估计量类，进行理论分析以确定其极小极大性条件。

Result: 建立了该类简单贝叶斯估计量的明确闭式极小极大性结果，为这类估计量的理论性质提供了完整的理论支撑。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了前作遗留的问题，为Maruyama和Strawderman(2006)提出的广义贝叶斯估计量类提供了完整的极小极大性理论框架，完善了正态方差估计的理论体系。

Abstract: This paper is a follow-up to Maruyama and Strawderman (2006, Journal of Statistical Planning and Inference), which identified a new class of generalized Bayes estimators with a particularly simple form for estimating a normal variance under entropy loss. Although their previous work established the Bayesianity of these estimators, it did not provide a closed-form result for their minimaxity. In this paper, we revisit the problem and establish a definitive closed-form minimaxity result for this class of simple Bayes estimators.

</details>


### [278] [Predictive Coherence and the Moment Hierarchy: Martingale Posteriors for Exchangeable Bernoulli Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00661)
*Nicholas G. Polson,Daniel Zantedeschi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文证明在可交换伯努利序列中，仅凭后验均值无法唯一确定多步预测概率，需要完整的后验分布信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可交换伯努利序列中多步预测概率与后验分布的关系，特别是探究仅使用后验均值（如Fong等人的鞅后验框架）是否足以唯一确定多步预测分布。

Method: 通过二项式展开分析k步预测概率与后验矩的关系，证明从后验均值到k步预测的映射是集值映射。建立闭包定理，证明鞅后验确定所有k步预测当且仅当终端值的条件律被唯一指定。

Result: 对于k≥2，仅凭后验均值无法唯一确定k步预测概率，映射是集值的。在任意严格适当评分规则下，当后验非退化时，插件预测严格劣于贝叶斯预测。差异为O(Var(θ|F_n))，随着后验集中而消失。

Conclusion: 在可交换性下实现预测完备性需要完整的后验分布信息，而不仅仅是后验均值。Hill的A_(n)规则在Jeffreys Beta(1/2,1/2)先验下是正面例子，但一般情况下需要更强的结构要求。

Abstract: For an exchangeable Bernoulli sequence with de Finetti mixing measure Pi, the k-step predictive probability P(X_{n+1}=...=X_{n+k}=0 | F_n) equals the posterior expectation E[(1-theta)^k | F_n]. By binomial expansion, this depends on all posterior moments up to order k. We show that the first moment alone is not sufficient to uniquely identify these quantities: for k >= 2, the mapping from posterior mean to k-step predictive is set-valued. The martingale posterior framework of Fong, Holmes, and Walker (which constrains only the first conditional moment of the terminal value) does not, in general, uniquely identify multi-step predictive distributions. Under any strictly proper scoring rule, the plug-in predictive is strictly dominated by the Bayes predictive whenever the posterior is non-degenerate. A closure theorem establishes that a martingale posterior determines all k-step predictives if and only if the conditional law of the terminal value is uniquely specified. Hill's A_(n) rule under the Jeffreys Beta(1/2,1/2) prior is a positive example. The discrepancy is O(Var(theta | F_n)) and vanishes as the posterior concentrates. These results clarify the structural requirements for predictive completeness under exchangeability.

</details>


### [279] [A simple integral representation of single-event scoring rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00750)
*Alexander R. Pruss*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个不涉及导数或连续性假设的简单积分表示，用于适当的单事件评分规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评分规则在概率预测评估中至关重要，但传统的表示方法通常需要导数或连续性假设。本文旨在为适当的单事件评分规则提供一个更简单、更基础的积分表示，减少对数学条件的依赖。

Method: 作者开发了一个积分表示方法，该表示不依赖于导数或连续性假设。该方法通过积分形式表达适当的单事件评分规则，避免了传统方法中常见的数学约束条件。

Result: 成功推导出了一个简单且通用的积分表示，适用于所有适当的单事件评分规则。该表示不需要任何导数或连续性假设，为评分规则的理论分析和实际应用提供了更灵活的基础。

Conclusion: 本文提出的积分表示为适当的单事件评分规则提供了一个简洁而强大的数学框架，简化了评分规则的理论分析，并为实际应用中的概率预测评估提供了更通用的工具。

Abstract: A simple integral representation involving no derivatives or continuity assumptions is given for proper single-event scoring rules.

</details>


### [280] [A New Look at the Visual Performance of Nonparametric Hazard Rate Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00794)
*Olaf Gefeller,Nils Lid Hjort*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 将视觉误差准则从密度估计扩展到删失数据的危险率估计，分析新准则与传统方法何时产生不同结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核方法非参数曲线估计中理论与应用存在冲突，特别是在估计器性能评估方面。Marron和Tsybakov提出的视觉误差准则解决了密度估计中的争议，需要将其扩展到危险率估计领域

Method: 将基于Hausdorff距离的视觉误差准则思想转移到删失数据的危险率估计中，使用欧氏距离的积分替代来测量两个集合的接近程度

Result: 能够推导出类似结果，有助于理解新准则何时会给出与传统方法不同的答案

Conclusion: 成功将视觉误差准则扩展到危险率估计领域，为评估估计器性能提供了新的理论框架，有助于弥合理论与应用之间的差距

Abstract: Nonparametric curve estimation by kernel methods has attracted widespread interest in theoretical and applied statistics. One area of conflict between theory and application relates to the evaluation of the performance of the estimators. Recently, Marron and Tsybakov (1995) proposed {\it visual error criteria} for addressing this issue of controversy in density estimation. Their core idea consists in using integrated alternatives to the Hausdorff distance for measuring the closeness of two sets based onthe Euclidean distance. In this paper, we transfer these ideas to hazard rate estimation from censored data. We are able to derive similar results that help to understand when the application of the new criteria will lead to answers that differ from those given by the conventional approaches.

</details>


### [281] [Minimax convergence rates of a binary plug-in type classification procedure for time-homogeneous SDE paths under low-noise conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00827)
*Eddy Michel Ella-Mintsa*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了SDE路径分类问题，在更复杂的扩散模型下建立了比现有文献更快的收敛速率，并分析了经验分类器的风险下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中关于SDE路径分类的极小极大收敛速率研究很少，只有一篇论文针对白噪声模型建立了最优收敛速率。本文旨在研究更复杂的扩散模型，其中漂移系数依赖于类别且扩散系数为所有类别共有，以探索更快的收敛速率。

Method: 采用基于空间依赖漂移和扩散系数的复杂扩散模型，在低噪声条件下建立指数不等式，这是获得期望收敛速率的关键。然后研究经验分类器的超额风险下界。

Result: 在低噪声条件下，在Hölder空间上建立了比现有文献更快的收敛速率。通过建立指数不等式获得了期望的收敛速率，并分析了经验分类器的超额风险下界。

Conclusion: 本文在更复杂的扩散模型下成功建立了SDE路径分类的快速收敛速率，填补了该领域的研究空白，为SDE路径分类的理论分析提供了新的工具和结果。

Abstract: The study of minimax convergence rates for classification procedures adapted to SDE paths is rarely addressed in the literature. Only one paper established optimal convergence rates for a binary classifier for SDE paths constructed from the white noise model. In this paper, we consider a more complex diffusion model with space-dependent drift and diffusion coefficients where the drift depends on the class and the diffusion coefficient is common to all classes. We establish, under the low-noise condition, a faster convergence rate over a Holder space. This result will require the establishment of an exponential inequality, which is essential to obtain the expected rate. We then study the lower bound on the excess risk of the empirical classifier.

</details>


### [282] [A new kernel-based index for the global sensitivity analysis of models with correlated inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00849)
*Troy Larsen,Alen Alexanderian*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出基于HSIC的全局敏感性分析方法，适用于具有相关性和函数值输入输出的模型，定义了总HSIC敏感性指数作为总效应Sobol指数的有界、可解释、矩独立的类比


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种适用于具有相关输入和函数值输出的模型的全局敏感性分析方法，传统Sobol指数在处理相关输入和函数值输出时存在局限性

Method: 定义总HSIC敏感性指数，利用增强核类建立HSIC的边际化单调性，开发高效算法计算HSIC经验估计器以降低计算复杂度和存储需求

Result: 建立了HSIC的单调性性质，提供了高效的计算算法，通过计算实验验证了总HSIC敏感性指数在非线性关系、相关输入和函数输出模型中的有效性和可解释性

Conclusion: HSIC方法为具有相关输入和函数值输出的模型提供了一种有效的全局敏感性分析框架，总HSIC敏感性指数具有有界性、可解释性和矩独立性等理想特性

Abstract: We present an HSIC-based approach for global sensitivity analysis of broad classes of models with correlated and possibly function-valued inputs and outputs. To this end, we define the total HSIC sensitivity index: a bounded, interpretable, and moment-independent analogue to the total-effect Sobol' index. These desirable qualities hinge upon the key property of monotonicity under marginalization for the HSIC. We rigorously establish this monotonicity property by using a suitable class of augmented kernels. Furthermore, we provide an efficient algorithm for computing an empirical estimator of the HSIC that significantly reduces computational complexity and storage requirements. The effectiveness and interpretability of the total HSIC sensitivity indices are demonstrated through computational experiments on models that feature nonlinear relationships, correlated inputs, and functional outputs.

</details>


### [283] [A Laplace-based perspective on conditional mean risk sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01434)
*Christopher Blier-Wong*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出基于联合拉普拉斯-斯蒂尔切斯变换计算条件均值风险分担规则的新框架，避免复杂多维积分，通过偏导数和一维拉普拉斯反演获得分配函数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件均值风险分担规则在连续多元模型中通常需要复杂的多维积分计算，现有方法在高维设置下不可行，需要更高效的计算框架

Method: 从风险向量的联合拉普拉斯-斯蒂尔切斯变换出发，将分配测度的LST表示为联合LST在对角线上的偏导数；当密度存在时，通过一维拉普拉斯反演获得f_S和ξ_i，进而计算h_i(s)=ξ_i(s)/f_S(s)；引入指数倾斜法稳定低概率聚合事件的数值反演

Result: 为广泛分布类别提供闭式或半解析解，开发了数值反演方法处理无法解析反演的情况，在高维设置中展示了方法的可行性

Conclusion: 基于LST的框架显著简化了CMRS分配的计算，避免了复杂多维积分，为高维风险分担问题提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: The conditional mean risk-sharing (CMRS) rule is an important tool for distributing aggregate losses across individual risks, but its implementation in continuous multivariate models typically requires complicated multidimensional integrals. We develop a framework to compute CMRS allocations from the joint Laplace--Stieltjes transform of the risk vector. The LSTs of the allocation measures $ν_i(B)=\mathbb{E}[X_i\boldsymbol{1}_{\{S\in B\}}]$ are expressed as partial derivatives of the joint LST evaluated on the diagonal $t_1=\cdots=t_n$. When densities exist, this yields one-dimensional Laplace inversions for $f_S$ and $ξ_i$, and hence $h_i(s)=ξ_i(s)/f_S(s)$ on the absolutely continuous part, providing closed-form or semi-analytic solutions for a broad class of distributions. We also develop numerical inversion methods for cases where analytic inversion is unavailable. We introduce an exponential tilting procedure to stabilize numerical inversion in low-probability aggregate events. We provide several examples to illustrate the approach, including in some high-dimensional settings where existing approaches are infeasible.

</details>


### [284] [Wasserstein-based identification of metastable states in time series data via change point detection and segment clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01989)
*David Gentile,Joshua Huang,James M. Murphy*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Wasserstein距离的无监督变点检测方法，用于多维时间序列的分割和聚类，无需参数假设，计算复杂度线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分析中的变点检测是一个重要但困难的统计问题，现有方法存在局限性。需要一种能够处理多维数据、无需参数假设且计算高效的方法来识别时间序列中的结构变化和聚类。

Method: 利用Wasserstein度量作为工具，在一维情况下利用最优传输成本的简单性，通过估计Wasserstein度量导数来识别变点，然后使用Wasserstein距离作为相似性度量对识别出的片段进行聚类。

Result: 方法在合成数据和真实数据（分子动力学模拟和水下声学）上测试成功，能够识别过程中的亚稳态。算法在数据大小和样本维度上具有线性计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 基于Wasserstein度量的变点检测方法为多维时间序列分析提供了一种有效、无参数、计算高效的解决方案，能够成功识别时间序列中的结构变化和聚类模式。

Abstract: Change point detection for time series analysis is a difficult and important problem in applied statistics, for which a variety of approaches have been developed in the past several decades. Here, the Wasserstein metric is employed as a tool for change-point identification in multi-dimensional time series data in order to identify clusters in time series in an unsupervised way. We leverage the simplicity of the optimal transport cost in the 1-dimensional setting to quickly identify both a segmentation (family of change points for a trajectory) and a clustering for the data when the number of segments is much smaller than the number of data points, making no parametric assumptions about the particular distributions involved. Our change point detection method scales linearly in the size of the data and in the dimension of the samples. We test our approach on idealized synthetic data trajectories, as well as real world trajectories coming from the domain of molecular dynamics simulations and underwater acoustics. We find that segmenting these time series via change points obtained by estimating the Wasserstein metric derivative and then clustering the identified segments as measures with similarity measured by the Wasserstein metric, successfully identifies metastable states in the law of the processes.

</details>


### [285] [Algebraic statistics of Hüsler-Reiss graphical models in multivariate extremes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02191)
*Carlos Améndola,Jane Ivy Coons,Alexandros Grosdos,Frank Röttger*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文从代数几何角度研究Hüsler-Reiss极值图模型，将其条件独立性关系转化为多项式约束，定义极值CI理想并找到其生成元的行列式表示，同时研究极值最大似然度和阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极值统计中的Hüsler-Reiss图模型，该模型是极值设定下多元高斯模型的类似物。从代数几何视角分析这些模型，探索其与高斯图模型的相似性和差异。

Method: 将条件独立性关系转化为参数的多项式约束，定义极值CI理想并找到其生成元的行列式表示。在参数推断方面，将极值最大似然度研究为条件负定矩阵完备问题的解的数量。定义并分析Hüsler-Reiss图模型的极值最大似然阈值。

Result: 获得了极值CI理想的生成元的行列式表示。将极值最大似然度与条件负定矩阵完备问题的解的数量联系起来。定义了极值最大似然阈值，该阈值提供了替代MLE存在的证书，基于实现基础汇总统计为欧几里得距离矩阵的点配置的维度。

Conclusion: 该研究从代数几何角度深入分析了Hüsler-Reiss极值图模型，揭示了其与高斯图模型在许多有趣方面的相似性和差异，为极值统计中的图模型提供了新的理论框架和分析工具。

Abstract: The field of extreme value statistics is concerned with modeling and predicting rare events. In a Hüsler-Reiss graphical model, a graph represents extremal conditional independence (CI) relations between random variables. These models are exponential families parameterized by a graph Laplacian and are considered the analogue of multivariate Gaussian models in the extremal setting. We study these models from the perspective of algebraic geometry. Translating the CI relations into polynomial constraints in the parameters, we define extremal CI ideals and find a determinantal representation of their generators. In terms of parametric inference, we study the extremal maximum likelihood degree as the number of solutions to a conditionally negative definite matrix completion problem. We also define and analyze the extremal maximum likelihood threshold for Hüsler-Reiss graphical models, which provides a certificate for the existence of a surrogate MLE in terms of the dimensionality of the point configuration that realizes the underlying summary statistic as a Euclidean distance matrix. We highlight throughout many interesting similarities but also differences with respect to Gaussian graphical models.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Near-Optimal Regret for KL-Regularized Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02155)
*Kaixuan Ji,Qingyue Zhao,Heyang Zhao,Qiwei Di,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对KL正则化多臂老虎机进行了尖锐分析，首次获得了线性依赖臂数K的高概率遗憾上界，并建立了匹配的下界，全面刻画了不同正则化强度下的遗憾行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管KL正则化强化学习已被证明能获得更快的收敛速度或对数遗憾，但其在线学习的统计效率仍未完全表征，即使在多臂老虎机这一简单场景中也是如此。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用新颖的剥离论证对KL-UCB算法进行尖锐分析，通过微妙的困难实例构造和定制的贝叶斯先验分解来建立下界，全面研究不同正则化强度下的遗憾行为。

Result: 获得了首个线性依赖臂数K的高概率遗憾上界$\tilde{O}(ηK\log^2T)$，并建立了匹配的下界$Ω(ηK \log T)$。在低正则化区域，遗憾为$\tildeΘ(\sqrt{KT})$且与η无关。

Conclusion: 本文全面理解了KL正则化多臂老虎机在所有正则化强度下的行为，在K、η和T方面获得了近乎最优的界限，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that reinforcement learning with KL-regularized objectives can enjoy faster rates of convergence or logarithmic regret, in contrast to the classical $\sqrt{T}$-type regret in the unregularized setting. However, the statistical efficiency of online learning with respect to KL-regularized objectives remains far from completely characterized, even when specialized to multi-armed bandits (MABs). We address this problem for MABs via a sharp analysis of KL-UCB using a novel peeling argument, which yields a $\tilde{O}(ηK\log^2T)$ upper bound: the first high-probability regret bound with linear dependence on $K$. Here, $T$ is the time horizon, $K$ is the number of arms, $η^{-1}$ is the regularization intensity, and $\tilde{O}$ hides all logarithmic factors except those involving $\log T$. The near-tightness of our analysis is certified by the first non-constant lower bound $Ω(ηK \log T)$, which follows from subtle hard-instance constructions and a tailored decomposition of the Bayes prior. Moreover, in the low-regularization regime (i.e., large $η$), we show that the KL-regularized regret for MABs is $η$-independent and scales as $\tildeΘ(\sqrt{KT})$. Overall, our results provide a thorough understanding of KL-regularized MABs across all regimes of $η$ and yield nearly optimal bounds in terms of $K$, $η$, and $T$.

</details>


### [287] [StaTS: Spectral Trajectory Schedule Learning for Adaptive Time Series Forecasting with Frequency Guided Denoiser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00037)
*Jintao Zhang,Zirui Liu,Mingyue Cheng,Xianquan Wang,Zhiding Liu,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StaTS是一种用于概率时间序列预测的扩散模型，通过学习噪声调度和去噪器来改善结构保持和频谱恢复


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型使用固定噪声调度会导致中间状态难以反转、终端状态偏离近噪声假设，且现有方法主要依赖时域条件而很少建模调度引起的频谱退化，限制了跨噪声级别的结构恢复

Method: 提出StaTS模型，包含：1) 频谱轨迹调度器(STS)学习数据自适应噪声调度，通过频谱正则化改善结构保持和逐步可逆性；2) 频率引导去噪器(FGD)估计调度引起的频谱失真，用于调制去噪强度以实现跨扩散步骤和变量的异质恢复；3) 两阶段训练程序稳定调度学习和去噪器优化之间的耦合

Result: 在多个真实世界基准测试中显示出一致的性能提升，同时在使用较少采样步骤时仍保持强大性能

Conclusion: StaTS通过学习自适应噪声调度和频率引导去噪，有效解决了传统扩散模型在时间序列预测中的结构保持和频谱恢复问题，实现了更好的概率预测性能

Abstract: Diffusion models have been used for probabilistic time series forecasting and show strong potential. However, fixed noise schedules often produce intermediate states that are hard to invert and a terminal state that deviates from the near noise assumption. Meanwhile, prior methods rely on time domain conditioning and seldom model schedule induced spectral degradation, which limits structure recovery across noise levels. We propose StaTS, a diffusion model for probabilistic time series forecasting that learns the noise schedule and the denoiser through alternating updates. StaTS includes Spectral Trajectory Scheduler (STS) that learns a data adaptive noise schedule with spectral regularization to improve structural preservation and stepwise invertibility, and Frequency Guided Denoiser (FGD) that estimates schedule induced spectral distortion and uses it to modulate denoising strength for heterogeneous restoration across diffusion steps and variables. A two stage training procedure stabilizes the coupling between schedule learning and denoiser optimization. Experiments on multiple real world benchmarks show consistent gains, while maintaining strong performance with fewer sampling steps. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjt-gpu/StaTS/.

</details>


### [288] [CARE: Confounder-Aware Aggregation for Reliable LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00039)
*Jitian Zhao,Changho Shin,Tzu-Heng Huang,Satya Sai Srinath Namburi GNVV,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE框架解决LLM评委聚合中的相关误差问题，通过显式建模潜在真实质量信号和共享混淆因素，提高评估准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-a-judge集成评估方法存在根本缺陷：假设评委提供独立的质量估计，但实际上LLM评委存在由共享潜在混淆因素（如冗长性、风格偏好、训练伪影）导致的相关误差，导致多数投票或平均等标准聚合规则增益有限甚至放大系统性错误

Method: 提出CARE（混淆因素感知聚合框架），显式建模LLM评委分数来自潜在真实质量信号和共享混淆因素，无需真实标签即可分离质量与混淆因素，提供可识别性和有限样本恢复的理论保证

Result: 在12个公共基准测试（连续评分、二元分类和成对偏好设置）中，CARE提高了聚合准确性，将误差降低了高达26.8%

Conclusion: CARE通过显式建模共享混淆因素解决了LLM评委聚合中的相关误差问题，提供了理论保证并在多个评估场景中显著提高了准确性，代码已开源

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge ensembles are the standard paradigm for scalable evaluation, but their aggregation mechanisms suffer from a fundamental flaw: they implicitly assume that judges provide independent estimates of true quality. However, in practice, LLM judges exhibit correlated errors caused by shared latent confounders -- such as verbosity, stylistic preferences, or training artifacts -- causing standard aggregation rules like majority vote or averaging to provide little gain or even amplify systematic mistakes. To address this, we introduce CARE, a confounder-aware aggregation framework that explicitly models LLM judge scores as arising from both a latent true-quality signal and shared confounding factors. Rather than heuristically re-weighting judges, CARE separates quality from confounders without access to ground-truth labels. We provide theoretical guarantees for identifiability and finite-sample recovery under shared confounders, and we quantify the systematic bias incurred when aggregation models omit confounding latent factors. Across 12 public benchmarks spanning continuous scoring, binary classification, and pairwise preference settings, CARE improves aggregation accuracy, reducing error by up to 26.8\%. Code is released in \href{https://github.com/SprocketLab/CARE}{https://github.com/SprocketLab/CARE}.

</details>


### [289] [Attn-QAT: 4-Bit Attention With Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00040)
*Peiyuan Zhang,Matthew Noto,Wenxuan Tan,Chengquan Jiang,Will Lin,Wei Zhou,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次系统研究注意力机制的4位量化感知训练，提出Attn-QAT方法解决FP4注意力训练不稳定问题，在扩散和语言模型中恢复FP4注意力质量下降，实现1.5倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现可靠的4位注意力是新兴FP4 GPU上端到端FP4计算的前提，但由于FP4动态范围极小且注意力激活具有重尾分布，注意力成为主要障碍。现有"即插即用"QAT方法（FP4前向+高精度Flash Attention后向）会导致训练不稳定

Method: 提出Attn-QAT方法，基于两个关键原则：1）在后向传播中匹配低精度注意力分数重计算；2）解决Flash Attention梯度计算中的隐式精度假设。实现融合Triton内核用于训练和FP4推理内核

Result: 在扩散模型和语言模型中，Attn-QAT恢复了FP4注意力的质量下降，无需先前FP4注意力中使用的显式离群值缓解启发式方法，在RTX 5090上实现高达1.5倍加速

Conclusion: 通过系统研究FP4注意力量化，提出了稳定训练的关键原则和Attn-QAT方法，为新兴FP4 GPU上的高效端到端4位计算提供了可行解决方案

Abstract: Achieving reliable 4-bit attention is a prerequisite for end-to-end FP4 computation on emerging FP4-capable GPUs, yet attention remains the main obstacle due to FP4's tiny dynamic range and attention's heavy-tailed activations. This paper presents the first systematic study of 4-bit quantization-aware training (QAT) for attention. We find that "drop-in" QAT, which naively combines an FP4 forward pass with a high-precision Flash Attention (FA)-style backward pass, leads to training instability. We identify two key principles for stable FP4 attention: (1) matching low-precision recomputation of attention scores in the backward pass, and (2) resolving implicit precision assumptions in FA's gradient calculation. Based on these insights, we propose Attn-QAT and implement fused Triton kernels for training as well as FP4 inference kernels. Across diffusion and language models, Attn-QAT recovers the quality drop from FP4 attention without explicit outlier-mitigation heuristics used in prior FP4 attention, and delivers up to a 1.5x speedup on an RTX 5090. Video demos can be found at https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/190F6xbBDUF2kGQYIcXBt3ehSYij5jlim?usp=sharing.

</details>


### [290] [Econometric vs. Causal Structure-Learning for Time-Series Policy Decisions: Evidence from the UK COVID-19 Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00041)
*Bruno Petrungaro,Anthony C. Constantinou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了计量经济学方法和传统因果机器学习算法在时间序列因果发现方面的性能，评估了4种计量经济学方法和11种因果ML算法，使用英国COVID-19案例作为实际应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前因果机器学习主要关注横截面数据，而时间序列数据的因果结构恢复仍是研究热点。计量经济学在时间序列因果关系方面有长期研究积累，需要比较计量经济学方法与因果ML算法的性能，探索两者相互借鉴的可能性。

Method: 研究评估了4种计量经济学方法和11种因果ML算法，使用英国COVID-19真实案例。通过图形结构、模型维度和因果效应恢复能力三个维度进行比较，并开发代码将计量经济学方法结果转换为bnlearn库格式。

Result: 计量经济学方法提供清晰的时间结构规则，而因果ML算法通过探索更大的图结构空间实现更广泛的发现，倾向于生成更密集的图，捕获更多可识别的因果关系。

Conclusion: 计量经济学和因果ML在时间序列因果发现中各有优势，两者可以相互借鉴。计量经济学提供明确的时间结构规则，而因果ML提供更广泛的图结构探索。研究为政策决策支持提供了算法选择的参考依据。

Abstract: Causal machine learning (ML) recovers graphical structures that inform us about potential cause-and-effect relationships. Most progress has focused on cross-sectional data with no explicit time order, whereas recovering causal structures from time series data remains the subject of ongoing research in causal ML. In addition to traditional causal ML, this study assesses econometric methods that some argue can recover causal structures from time series data. The use of these methods can be explained by the significant attention the field of econometrics has given to causality, and specifically to time series, over the years. This presents the possibility of comparing the causal discovery performance between econometric and traditional causal ML algorithms. We seek to understand if there are lessons to be incorporated into causal ML from econometrics, and provide code to translate the results of these econometric methods to the most widely used Bayesian Network R library, bnlearn. We investigate the benefits and challenges that these algorithms present in supporting policy decision-making, using the real-world case of COVID-19 in the UK as an example. Four econometric methods are evaluated in terms of graphical structure, model dimensionality, and their ability to recover causal effects, and these results are compared with those of eleven causal ML algorithms. Amongst our main results, we see that econometric methods provide clear rules for temporal structures, whereas causal-ML algorithms offer broader discovery by exploring a larger space of graph structures that tends to lead to denser graphs that capture more identifiable causal relationships.

</details>


### [291] [Maximizing the Spectral Energy Gain in Sub-1-Bit LLMs via Latent Geometry Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00042)
*Banseok Lee,Youngmin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LittleBit-2通过内部潜在旋转和联合迭代量化解决低秩二进制近似中的潜在几何错位问题，在亚1比特压缩中实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现极端模型压缩中存在谱能量增益现象，即低秩二进制近似可能优于极小秩浮点基线，但现有方法因潜在几何错位问题未能实现这一潜力

Method: 提出LittleBit-2框架，采用内部潜在旋转作为几何预处理器，结合联合迭代量化，将相干潜在分布与二进制超立方体对齐，实现零推理开销

Result: 在Llama-2和Llama-3模型上，在1~0.1 bpp的亚1比特压缩范围内建立了新的SOTA，匹配领先的1比特基线模型的保真度

Conclusion: 通过解决潜在几何错位问题，LittleBit-2成功实现了谱能量增益，为极端模型压缩提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: We identify the Spectral Energy Gain in extreme model compression, where low-rank binary approximations outperform tiny-rank floating-point baselines for heavy-tailed spectra. However, prior attempts fail to realize this potential, trailing state-of-the-art 1-bit methods. We attribute this degradation to Latent Geometry Misalignment: standard singular vectors exhibit high coherence (spiky distribution), the worst-case geometry for binary quantization. To realize this gain, we propose LittleBit-2, a framework employing Internal Latent Rotation and Joint Iterative Quantization (Joint-ITQ). This approach acts as a geometric preconditioner, aligning coherent latent distributions with the binary hypercube with zero inference overhead. Empirically, LittleBit-2 establishes a new state-of-the-art in the sub-1-bit regime (1$\sim$0.1 bpp) on Llama-2 and Llama-3, matching the fidelity of leading 1-bit baselines.

</details>


### [292] [Reinforcement Learning for Control with Probabilistic Stability Guarantee: A Finite-Sample Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00043)
*Minghao Han,Lixian Zhang,Chenliang Liu,Zhipeng Zhou,Jun Wang,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种具有概率稳定性保证的强化学习方法，利用有限数据通过Lyapunov方法确保均方稳定性，并开发了L-REINFORCE算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合强化学习与控制理论之间的关键差距，在模型无关框架下使用有限数据进行稳定性分析和控制器设计。

Method: 基于Lyapunov方法提出概率稳定性定理，推导稳定策略学习的策略梯度定理，开发扩展REINFORCE算法的L-REINFORCE算法。

Result: L-REINFORCE在Cartpole任务仿真中优于基线方法，能有效确保稳定性，稳定性概率随轨迹数量和长度增加而提高。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将强化学习与控制理论结合，为模型无关框架下的稳定性保证提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to reinforcement learning (RL) for control systems that provides probabilistic stability guarantees using finite data. Leveraging Lyapunov's method, we propose a probabilistic stability theorem that ensures mean square stability using only a finite number of sampled trajectories. The probability of stability increases with the number and length of trajectories, converging to certainty as data size grows. Additionally, we derive a policy gradient theorem for stabilizing policy learning and develop an RL algorithm, L-REINFORCE, that extends the classical REINFORCE algorithm to stabilization problems. The effectiveness of L-REINFORCE is demonstrated through simulations on a Cartpole task, where it outperforms the baseline in ensuring stability. This work bridges a critical gap between RL and control theory, enabling stability analysis and controller design in a model-free framework with finite data.

</details>


### [293] [REMIND: Rethinking Medical High-Modality Learning under Missingness--A Long-Tailed Distribution Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00046)
*Chenwei Wu,Zitao Shuai,Liyue Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REMIND框架从长尾分布视角重新思考高模态缺失下的多模态学习，通过组专业化混合专家架构和组分布鲁棒优化解决尾部模态组合性能不佳问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实临床应用中，由于数据收集限制，患者通常无法获得完整模态观测，导致"高模态缺失学习"问题。这种缺失导致模态组合呈指数增长，并形成长尾分布，而现有方法忽视了这一关键现象，导致尾部模态组合组性能显著下降

Method: 提出REMIND统一框架：1) 组专业化混合专家架构，可扩展地学习任意模态组合的组特定多模态融合函数；2) 组分布鲁棒优化策略，对代表性不足的模态组合进行加权

Result: 在真实世界医学数据集上的广泛实验表明，该框架始终优于最先进方法，并在高模态缺失下的各种医学多模态学习应用中具有稳健的泛化能力

Conclusion: 从长尾分布视角重新思考高模态缺失下的多模态学习至关重要，REMIND通过解决梯度不一致和概念偏移问题，有效提升了尾部模态组合的性能，为临床多模态学习提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Medical multi-modal learning is critical for integrating information from a large set of diverse modalities. However, when leveraging a high number of modalities in real clinical applications, it is often impractical to obtain full-modality observations for every patient due to data collection constraints, a problem we refer to as 'High-Modality Learning under Missingness'. In this study, we identify that such missingness inherently induces an exponential growth in possible modality combinations, followed by long-tail distributions of modality combinations due to varying modality availability. While prior work overlooked this critical phenomenon, we find this long-tailed distribution leads to significant underperformance on tail modality combination groups. Our empirical analysis attributes this problem to two fundamental issues: 1) gradient inconsistency, where tail groups' gradient updates diverge from the overall optimization direction; 2) concept shifts, where each modality combination requires distinct fusion functions. To address these challenges, we propose REMIND, a unified framework that REthinks MultImodal learNing under high-moDality missingness from a long-tail perspective. Our core idea is to propose a novel group-specialized Mixture-of-Experts architecture that scalably learns group-specific multi-modal fusion functions for arbitrary modality combinations, while simultaneously leveraging a group distributionally robust optimization strategy to upweight underrepresented modality combinations. Extensive experiments on real-world medical datasets show that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, and robustly generalizes across various medical multi-modal learning applications under high-modality missingness.

</details>


### [294] [BiJEPA: Bi-directional Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture for Symmetric Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00049)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiJEPA：一种双向联合嵌入预测架构，通过循环一致性预测和范数正则化解决标准JEPA单向预测的局限性，在多种模态数据上实现稳定收敛和鲁棒表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JEPA模型采用单向预测机制（上下文→目标），可能忽略反向关系中的信息信号，导致性能下降。需要更全面的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出BiJEPA（双向联合嵌入预测架构），强制数据片段间的循环一致性可预测性。引入关键范数正则化机制解决对称预测中的表示爆炸不稳定问题。

Result: 在三种不同模态上评估：合成周期信号、混沌Lorenz吸引子轨迹和高维图像数据（MNIST）。BiJEPA实现稳定收敛无崩溃，捕获混沌系统的语义结构，学习到能够生成和泛化的鲁棒时空表示。

Conclusion: BiJEPA通过双向预测和正则化机制，为表示学习提供了更全面的方法，解决了标准JEPA的局限性，在多种数据模态上表现出色。

Abstract: Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) has shifted from pixel-level reconstruction to latent space prediction, spearheaded by the Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA). While effective, standard JEPA models typically rely on a uni-directional prediction mechanism (e.g. Context $\to$ Target), potentially neglecting the informative signal inherent in the inverse relationship, degrading its performance. In this work, we propose \textbf{BiJEPA}, a \textit{Bi-Directional Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture} that enforces cycle-consistent predictability between data segments. We address the inherent instability of symmetric prediction (representation explosion) by introducing a critical norm regularization mechanism on the representation vectors. We evaluate BiJEPA on three distinct modalities: synthetic periodic signals, chaotic Lorenz attractor trajectories, and high-dimensional image data (MNIST). Our results demonstrate that BiJEPA achieves stable convergence without collapse, captures the semantic structure of chaotic systems, and learns robust temporal and spatial representations capable of generation and generalisation, offering a more holistic approach to representation learning.

</details>


### [295] [Mag-Mamba: Modeling Coupled spatiotemporal Asymmetry for POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00053)
*Zhuoxuan Li,Tangwei Ye,Jieyuan Pei,Haina Liang,Zhongyuan Lai,Zihan Liu,Yiming Wu,Qi Zhang,Liang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mag-Mamba框架通过将时空不对称性建模为复数域中的相位驱动旋转动力学，解决了POI推荐中的时空不对称挑战，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有POI推荐方法面临时空不对称性的根本挑战：位置间的转移意图具有高度不对称性且随时间动态变化。传统方法依赖对称算子或实值聚合，难以统一建模时间变化的全局方向性。

Method: 提出Mag-Mamba框架：1) 时间条件磁相位编码器，在邻接图上构建时间条件磁拉普拉斯算子，利用边相位差表征全局演化的空间方向性；2) 复数Mamba模块，将传统标量状态衰减推广为联合衰减-旋转动力学，由时间间隔和磁地理先验共同调制。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，Mag-Mamba相比最先进的基线方法取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将时空不对称性建模为复数域中的相位驱动旋转动力学，Mag-Mamba有效解决了POI推荐中的时空不对称挑战，为建模时间变化的全局方向性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation is a critical task in location-based services, yet it faces the fundamental challenge of coupled spatiotemporal asymmetry inherent in urban mobility. Specifically, transition intents between locations exhibit high asymmetry and are dynamically conditioned on time. Existing methods, typically built on graph or sequence backbones, rely on symmetric operators or real-valued aggregations, struggling to unify the modeling of time-varying global directionality. To address this limitation, we propose Mag-Mamba, a framework whose core insight lies in modeling spatiotemporal asymmetry as phase-driven rotational dynamics in the complex domain. Based on this, we first devise a Time-conditioned Magnetic Phase Encoder that constructs a time-conditioned Magnetic Laplacian on the geographic adjacency graph, utilizing edge phase differences to characterize the globally evolving spatial directionality. Subsequently, we introduce a Complex-valued Mamba module that generalizes traditional scalar state decay into joint decay-rotation dynamics, explicitly modulated by both time intervals and magnetic geographic priors. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that Mag-Mamba achieves significant performance improvements over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [296] [Expert Divergence Learning for MoE-based Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00054)
*Jiaang Li,Haibin Chen,Langming Liu,Yujin Yuan,Yadao Wang,Yizhen Zhang,Chengting Yu,Xin Tong,Weidong Zhang,Shilei Liu,Wenbo Su,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Expert Divergence Learning方法，通过标签驱动的辅助损失函数，利用预训练语料中的领域标签最大化不同数据领域间专家路由分布的Jensen-Shannon散度，以解决MoE架构中的专家同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家（MoE）架构是扩展语言模型的有效技术，但常面临专家同质化问题，即专家学习冗余功能，限制了MoE的潜力。需要一种方法来鼓励专家间的功能专业化。

Method: 提出Expert Divergence Learning预训练策略，引入标签驱动的辅助损失函数，利用预训练语料中的领域标签，最大化不同数据领域间专家路由分布的Jensen-Shannon散度，引导模型为不同领域开发不同的路由策略，为相同领域开发相似的路由策略。

Result: 在高达150亿参数的MoE模型上进行预训练验证，实验结果显示：1）获得更低的语言建模损失；2）在多样化下游基准测试中表现显著提升；3）有效缓解专家同质化问题，带来更强的功能专业化；4）训练期间计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: Expert Divergence Learning是一种有效的预训练策略，能够解决MoE架构中的专家同质化问题，促进专家功能专业化，提升模型性能，且计算开销极小。

Abstract: The Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is a powerful technique for scaling language models, yet it often suffers from expert homogenization, where experts learn redundant functionalities, thereby limiting MoE's full potential. To address this, we introduce Expert Divergence Learning, a novel pre-training strategy that explicitly encourages functional specialization among experts. Our method incorporates a label-driven auxiliary loss that leverages domain labels inherent in pre-training corpora to maximize the Jensen-Shannon Divergence between the expert routing distributions of different data domains. This optimization objective guides the model to develop diverged routing policies for varied domains and closer routing policies for the same domain, which leads to emergent and organized expert specialization. We validate our approach by pre-training MoE models of up to 15 billion parameters from scratch. Experimental results demonstrate that models trained with Expert Divergence Learning not only achieve a lower language modeling loss but also exhibit significant performance improvements across a diverse range of downstream benchmarks. Further analysis confirms that our method effectively mitigates expert homogenization and brings greater functional specialization, all with negligible computational overhead during training.

</details>


### [297] [M3-AD: Reflection-aware Multi-modal, Multi-category, and Multi-dimensional Benchmark and Framework for Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00055)
*Chao Huang,Yanhui Li,Yunkang Cao,Wei Wang,Hongxi Huang,Jie Wen,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M3-AD是一个用于工业异常检测的反射感知多模态框架，通过RA-Monitor组件实现可控自校正，解决MLLMs在复杂工业场景中高置信度但不可靠决策的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在工业异常检测中虽然实现了零样本范式，但在细粒度和结构复杂的工业场景中容易产生高置信度但不可靠的决策，且缺乏有效的自校正机制。

Method: 提出M3-AD框架，包含两个互补数据资源：用于反射对齐微调的M3-AD-FT和用于系统跨类别评估的M3-AD-Bench。在此基础上提出RA-Monitor，将反射建模为可学习的决策修订过程，指导模型在初始判断不可靠时执行可控自校正。

Result: 在M3-AD-Bench上的大量实验表明，RA-Monitor在零样本异常检测和异常分析任务中优于多个开源和商业MLLMs。

Conclusion: M3-AD通过反射感知学习框架和RA-Monitor自校正机制，显著提升了工业异常检测的决策鲁棒性和可靠性，为复杂工业场景中的零样本异常检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced industrial anomaly detection toward a zero-shot paradigm, they still tend to produce high-confidence yet unreliable decisions in fine-grained and structurally complex industrial scenarios, and lack effective self-corrective mechanisms. To address this issue, we propose M3-AD, a unified reflection-aware multimodal framework for industrial anomaly detection. M3-AD comprises two complementary data resources: M3-AD-FT, designed for reflection-aligned fine-tuning, and M3-AD-Bench, designed for systematic cross-category evaluation, together providing a foundation for reflection-aware learning and reliability assessment. Building upon this foundation, we propose RA-Monitor, which models reflection as a learnable decision revision process and guides models to perform controlled self-correction when initial judgments are unreliable, thereby improving decision robustness. Extensive experiments conducted on M3-AD-Bench demonstrate that RA-Monitor outperforms multiple open-source and commercial MLLMs in zero-shot anomaly detection and anomaly analysis tasks. Code will be released at https://github.com/Yanhui-Lee/M3-AD.

</details>


### [298] [Certainty-Validity: A Diagnostic Framework for Discrete Commitment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00070)
*Datorien L. Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Certainty-Validity框架，揭示离散承诺系统中标准评估指标的缺陷，发现模型在模糊数据上存在"自信错误"的失败模式，并定义了83%模糊性天花板现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习评估指标（准确率、精确率、召回率、AUROC）假设所有错误等价，但这对离散承诺系统（选择{-W, 0, +W}承诺状态的架构）在认识论上存在缺陷。需要新的评估框架来区分模型在模糊数据上的不同行为模式。

Method: 引入Certainty-Validity框架，将模型性能分解为2x2矩阵，区分高/低确定性和有效/无效预测。在Fashion-MNIST、EMNIST和IMDB数据集上进行消融实验，分析离散架构在噪声基准上的"83%模糊性天花板"现象。

Result: 发现离散模型在模糊数据上存在Confident-Incorrect行为（模型在模糊数据中幻觉结构），而连续模型可以通过记忆纹理或统计噪声超越83%天花板。离散模型拒绝承诺模糊样本不是失败而是特征——模型在结构证据结束处停止。标准训练最终导致良性过拟合，使模型从Uncertain-Incorrect（适当怀疑）病理性地迁移到Confident-Incorrect（幻觉）。

Conclusion: 推理系统的"良好训练"不应由准确率定义，而应通过最大化Certainty-Validity Score来确保模型知道在哪里停止。离散模型的拒绝承诺行为实际上是合理的认知边界，而非性能缺陷。

Abstract: Standard evaluation metrics for machine learning -- accuracy, precision, recall, and AUROC -- assume that all errors are equivalent: a confident incorrect prediction is penalized identically to an uncertain one. For discrete commitment systems (architectures that select committed states {-W, 0, +W}), this assumption is epistemologically flawed. We introduce the Certainty-Validity (CVS) Framework, a diagnostic method that decomposes model performance into a 2x2 matrix distinguishing high/low certainty from valid/invalid predictions. This framework reveals a critical failure mode hidden by standard accuracy: Confident-Incorrect (CI) behavior, where models hallucinate structure in ambiguous data. Through ablation experiments on Fashion-MNIST, EMNIST, and IMDB, we analyze the "83% Ambiguity Ceiling" -- a stopping point where this specific discrete architecture consistently plateaus on noisy benchmarks. Unlike continuous models that can surpass this ceiling by memorizing texture or statistical noise, the discrete model refuses to commit to ambiguous samples. We show that this refusal is not a failure but a feature: the model stops where structural evidence ends. However, standard training on ambiguous data eventually forces Benign Overfitting, causing a pathological migration from Uncertain-Incorrect (appropriate doubt) to Confident-Incorrect (hallucination).
